# Exact SQL text of every query — extracted directly from Access (QueryDef.SQL)

Generated by a VBA macro the client ran inside `SZAPP.mdb` (loops through `CurrentDb.QueryDefs`, prints `.Name` and `.SQL`). This is the authoritative source for query logic — it contains complete JOIN/GROUP BY/HAVING clauses, unlike the `queries/*.txt` design-format export or automatic extraction via mdbtools (both lose part of the information). 898 queries total (automatically generated hidden queries with the `~sq_` prefix are excluded — those back combo/list boxes and subforms and are naturally reproduced through the corresponding React components, no need to port them by hand).

Table and column names inside each query's SQL body have been translated from the original Serbian/legacy Access names to the English names used in `schema-ddl-draft.sql` and `data-model.md`, using the same dictionary for all three files so they stay consistent. Query **names** (the line right after each `=====QUERY=====` marker) and all string/date literal values are left exactly as extracted — those are Access object identifiers and real data, not schema attributes. A handful of tables referenced by older queries (e.g. `Troskovi_VP`/`ExpenseTypeSubtype`, `Troskovi_Racuni`/`ExpenseInvoice`) no longer exist in the current `.mdb` snapshot in `data-model.md` — they are translated for readability but are not part of the live schema. Likewise, computed/aggregate result-column aliases (e.g. `SumOfPIZNOS`, `CountOfIDRacun`) are query-local output labels, not real table columns, and were intentionally left untranslated to avoid guessing wrong — the underlying column references they sum/count are translated.

```
=====QUERY=====
_DUPLIRAJPODATKE
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO ExpenseInvoice ( InvoiceNumber, InvoiceName, Note, Supplier, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, IznosE, IznosD, CalculationType, InvoiceMonth, S, L, G )
SELECT ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceNumber, ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceName, ExpenseInvoice.Note, ExpenseInvoice.Supplier, ExpenseInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, ExpenseInvoice.IznosE, ExpenseInvoice.IznosD, ExpenseInvoice.CalculationType, [nov Month] AS MR, ExpenseInvoice.S, ExpenseInvoice.L, ExpenseInvoice.G
FROM ExpenseInvoice;


=====QUERY=====
AAAAA
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceId, InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, SupplierInvoiceId, Name, CalculationType, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, Quantity, PriceEur, ExchangeRateNbs, Amount, TotalRsd, SortOrder, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, InvoiceAmount, K1xK2, K2xK3, K2xK4, K2xK5 )
SELECT 9999 AS Expr1, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceLine.Name, InvoiceLine.CalculationType, InvoiceLine.K1, InvoiceLine.K2, InvoiceLine.K3, InvoiceLine.K4, InvoiceLine.K5, -Quantity AS Expr2, InvoiceLine.PriceEur, InvoiceLine.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLine.Amount, InvoiceLine.TotalRsd, InvoiceLine.SortOrder, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, InvoiceLine.InvoiceAmount, InvoiceLine.K1xK2, InvoiceLine.K2xK3, InvoiceLine.K2xK4, InvoiceLine.K2xK5
FROM InvoiceLine
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceId)=34));


=====QUERY=====
AAAAAB
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceId )
SELECT 9999 AS Expr1, InvoiceLine.*
FROM InvoiceLine
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceId)=34));


=====QUERY=====
AdresaDostave
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, BuildingEntrance.Address, BuildingEntrance.Building, UnitType.PrintLabel, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Company.InvoicePostalCode, Company.InvoiceCity, BuildingEntrance.Entrance
FROM Company INNER JOIN (((Partner INNER JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN BuildingEntrance ON Unit.Entrance = BuildingEntrance.Entrance) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Company.CompanyId = BuildingEntrance.CompanyId
WHERE (((UnitType.UnitTypeId)=1 Or (UnitType.UnitTypeId)=2));

Analiza_ClearData
-----SQL-----
UPDATE AnalysisReportDefinition SET AnalysisReportDefinition.RowCount = 0, AnalysisReportDefinition.LastRunAt = Null;


=====QUERY=====
ArchiveMails
-----SQL-----
UPDATE SentEmail SET SentEmail.Archive = -1;

aZURIRANJEpARAMETARUgK
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, OcistiPozivNaBroj(Right(DocumentRef,Len(DocumentRef)-2)) AS Expr1, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Note, LedgerEntry.Parameters
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber)=2) AND ((LedgerEntry.Note) Is Not Null));


=====QUERY=====
AzuriranjeSkupstineUGK
-----SQL-----
UPDATE LedgerEntry INNER JOIN PartnerCompanyLink ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId=PartnerCompanyLink.PartnerId SET LedgerEntry.CompanyId = PartnerCompanyLink.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
AzuriranjeSkupstineUGK?67
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Company INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON Company.TaxId=LedgerEntry.Description SET LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = 3, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = 1, LedgerEntry.Date = #6/30/2012#, LedgerEntry.DueDate = #7/15/2012#;


=====QUERY=====
BenefitCount
-----SQL-----
SELECT BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId, Count(BenefitPartnerPeriod.IsUsed) AS CountOfUsed, Sum(BenefitPartnerPeriod.IsUsed) AS SumOfUsed
FROM BenefitPartnerPeriod
GROUP BY BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
BENEFIT-GRP
-----SQL-----
SELECT Benefit.EntryDate, Benefit.PartnerAccountingId, Benefit.PeriodYyMm
FROM Benefit
GROUP BY Benefit.EntryDate, Benefit.PartnerAccountingId, Benefit.PeriodYyMm;


=====QUERY=====
BENEFIT-GRP_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Count([BENEFIT-GRP].PartnerAccountingId) AS CountOfKupacID
SELECT Partner.CompanyId, [BENEFIT-GRP].PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, Count([BENEFIT-GRP].PartnerAccountingId) AS [Total Of EntryDate]
FROM [BENEFIT-GRP] INNER JOIN Partner ON [BENEFIT-GRP].PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY Partner.CompanyId, [BENEFIT-GRP].PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
PIVOT [BENEFIT-GRP].PeriodYyMm;


=====QUERY=====
BenefitHave
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Benefit.PartnerAccountingId, Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Benefit.IsUsed
FROM Benefit INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Benefit.PeriodYyMm = InvoiceBatch.Code
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Benefit.PartnerAccountingId, Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Benefit.IsUsed
HAVING (((Benefit.IsUsed)=0));


=====QUERY=====
BenefitiExport
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Company.Building, UnitType.Name, Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode AS Unit, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.K3, Partner.Name, Benefit.EntryDate
FROM Partner INNER JOIN (((Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Benefit ON Unit.UnitId = Benefit.UnitId) INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
WHERE (((Benefit.EntryDate) Is Not Null) AND ((UnitType.UnitTypeId)=1))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
BenefitiExport_All
-----SQL-----
SELECT Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Company.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Company.Building, UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode AS Unit, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.K3, Partner.Name, Benefit.IsUsed
FROM Partner INNER JOIN (((Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Benefit ON Unit.UnitId = Benefit.UnitId) INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
ORDER BY Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Company.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeCode;

BenefitiExport_GPMima

SELECT Unit.UnitTypeId, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.UnitId, BenefitExport.PartnerId, BenefitExport.CompanyId AS Expr1
FROM BenefitExport INNER JOIN Unit ON BenefitExport.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));

FROM Partner AS Kupac_1 INNER JOIN (((Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Benefit ON Unit.UnitId = Benefit.UnitId) ON Kupac_1.LegacyMasterId = Partner.PartnerId
ORDER BY UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Kupac_1.PartnerId;

=====QUERY=====
BenefitUpdateRacun
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceId, InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId, "Benefit FM " & First([SumOfUsed])+1 & "/" & First([CountOfUsed]) & " - " & GetBenefitText(BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId) & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & "Na racunu je primenjen popust na uslugu održavanja stanova i garažnih mesta." & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & "A discount has been applied to the service of maintenance of apartments and garage spaces." AS Text
FROM Invoice INNER JOIN (BenefitCount INNER JOIN (BenefitPartnerPeriod INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON BenefitPartnerPeriod.PeriodYyMm = InvoiceBatch.Code) ON BenefitCount.PartnerAccountingId = BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId) ON (Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) AND (Invoice.PartnerId = BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId)
GROUP BY Invoice.InvoiceId, InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, BenefitPartnerPeriod.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Copy Of Query308
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Month, InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeId
FROM ((Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceUnit ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceUnit.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Unit ON InvoiceUnit.UnitId = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.Month)="02") AND ((InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear)="2026") AND ((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=231) AND ((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));


=====QUERY=====
Copy Of Query85
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, Sum(GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SumOfDIZNOS) AS SumOfSumOfDIZNOS, Sum(GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SumOfPIZNOS) AS SumOfSumOfPIZNOS, GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SubAccount
FROM SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI ON (SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SupplierInvoiceId) AND (SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.CompanyId)
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SubAccount
HAVING (((SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)=101));


=====QUERY=====
dell_KILLSPCSTAVKE
-----SQL-----
DELETE InvoiceLine.*, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.QrCodeFlag, InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId
FROM InvoiceLine INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=107) AND ((Invoice.QrCodeFlag)=1) AND ((InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=17 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=18 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=19 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=20 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=52 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=24 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=25 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=26 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=27 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=53 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=38 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=39 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=40 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=41 Or (InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId)=54));


=====QUERY=====
Dobavljaci_Racun_List
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.AmountByCoefficientRsd, SupplierInvoice.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker AS MVR, SupplierInvoice.CollectionPriority, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, SubAccount.Name, TipRacunaDobavljac.Caption
FROM (((SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN Company ON SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN TipRacunaDobavljac ON SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId = TipRacunaDobavljac.SortIndex
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.CollectionPriority;


=====QUERY=====
DOBAVLJACI_RACUNI_SORT
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*
FROM SupplierInvoice
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber;


=====QUERY=====
DPO_FIX_TS1_K2040
-----SQL-----
UPDATE LedgerEntry SET LedgerEntry.DueDate = Date
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.DueDate) Is Null) AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=1));


=====QUERY=====
eMail_Partner
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId
FROM PartnerEmail
GROUP BY PartnerEmail.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
eMail_Partner_list
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId

=====QUERY=====
EmailoviPlot24
-----SQL-----
SELECT PLOT24EMAL.Unit, PLOT24EMAL.Company, Partner.PartnerId, PLOT24EMAL.PersonalEmail, PLOT24EMAL.FullName, Partner.Name, Partner.TaxId, Partner.RegistrationNumber, Partner.TaxId, Partner.RegistrationNumber, Partner.Address, Partner.LegacyMasterId, Unit.UnitId
FROM (Unit RIGHT JOIN PLOT24EMAL ON Unit.UnitTypeShortCode = PLOT24EMAL.Unit) LEFT JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_001-SUMA-STAVKE-IZVODA-NIJE-SUMA-GK
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, ExecutionDate & " / " & CompanyId & " / " & Name & " / " & PayerRecipientName AS Expr1, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, Company.Name, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, (Credit-Debit)-(Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount))=0 AS Kontrola
FROM (LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatementLine.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY BankStatementLine.Id, ExecutionDate & " / " & CompanyId & " / " & Name & " / " & PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, Company.Name, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, BankStatementLine.BankStatementId
HAVING ((((Credit-Debit)-(Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount))=0)=0))
ORDER BY BankStatementLine.LineNumber ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_002-STAVKE U GK SA POGRESNIM SKUPSTINAMA KUPCA
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, "ID-K: " & PartnerId & " / " & [Skustina_1].Name & " / GK SZ:" & CompanyId & " / " & Company.Name AS Expr1, Skustina_1.Name AS [Skupstina kupca], LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, Partner.Name, Company.Name AS [Skupstina izvoda], LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Company AS Skustina_1 ON Partner.CompanyId = Skustina_1.CompanyId
WHERE (((CompanyId=CompanyId)=0) AND ((Partner.CompanyId)<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_003-STAVKE IZVODA NA CEKANJU
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, ExecutionDate & " /  RB: " & LineNumber & ". /  SZ: " & CompanyId AS Expr1, BankStatementLine.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.JournalEntryLineNumber, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.BankAccountNumber, BankStatementLine.Origin, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.Code, BankStatementLine.PaymentReference, BankStatementLine.PaymentReferenceOut, BankStatementLine.PartnerAccountingId, BankStatementLine.IsPosted, BankStatementLine.IsIgnored, BankStatementLine.IsMatched
FROM BankStatementLine LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) Is Null))
ORDER BY BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_004-NEPOSTOJI-KUPAC-PREMA-GK
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*, Company.Name, Company.CompanyId
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Partner.PartnerId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_005-KUPAC-BEZ-NEKRETNINE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, PartnerId & " / " & Partner.Name & " /  SZ: " & CompanyId & " / " & Name AS Expr1, Partner.Name, Company.Name, Partner.CompanyId, Company.Name
FROM (Partner LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Partner.PartnerId)<8000) AND ((Unit.PartnerId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_006-IZVODISTAVKE-BEZ-IZVODA
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, ExecutionDate & " /  RB: " & LineNumber & ". /  SZ: " & BankStatementLine.CompanyId AS Expr1, BankStatementLine.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.JournalEntryLineNumber, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.BankAccountNumber, BankStatementLine.Origin, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.Code, BankStatementLine.PaymentReference, BankStatementLine.PaymentReferenceOut, BankStatementLine.PartnerAccountingId, BankStatementLine.IsPosted, BankStatementLine.IsIgnored, BankStatementLine.IsMatched
FROM BankStatementLine LEFT JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId
WHERE (((BankStatement.BankStatementId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_007-IZVODI-KOJI-IMAJU-R-A-NISU-R
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, BankStatement.Date & " / " & BankStatement.CompanyId & " / " & Name AS Expr1, BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.IsUnposted, BankStatement.JournalEntryId
FROM ((BankStatementLine LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((BankStatement.IsUnposted)=True) AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) Is Null))
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_008-IZVODI-SUMASTAVKI-KONTROLA
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit) AS SumOfZaduzenje, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje, (Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit)=BankStatement.Debit) And (Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit)=BankStatement.Credit) And (Count(BankStatementLine.Id)=(BankStatement.DebitJournalEntryId+BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId)) AS Ispravno, Count(BankStatementLine.Id) AS BrojStavki, (BankStatement.DebitJournalEntryId+BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId) AS KontrolniBrojStavki
FROM (BankStatement LEFT JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, (BankStatement.DebitJournalEntryId+BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId)
HAVING ((((Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit)=BankStatement.Debit) And (Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit)=BankStatement.Credit) And (Count(BankStatementLine.Id)=(BankStatement.DebitJournalEntryId+BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId)))=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_009_IZVOD-NEPOSTOJECA-SZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.BankStatementId, "IZVOD ID: " & BankStatementId & " / SZ : " & CompanyId & " / DATUM : " & Date AS Expr1
FROM BankStatement LEFT JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Company.CompanyId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_009-IZVOD-STAVKE-U-GK-POGRESNA-SZ-KORISNIKA
-----SQL-----
SELECT Skustina_1.Name AS [Skupstina kupca], LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name AS [Skupstina izvoda], LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Company AS Skustina_1 ON Partner.CompanyId = Skustina_1.CompanyId
WHERE (((CompanyId=CompanyId)=0) AND ((Partner.CompanyId)<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_010-STAVKE-GK-KORISNIKA-BEZ-SK
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, PartnerId & " / " & Name & " / " & "GK: " & Date & " / " & JournalEntryNumber AS Expr1, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_011-IZVOD-STAVKE-POGRESNA-SZ-U-GK
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, ExecutionDate & " / " & CompanyId & " / " & Name AS Expr2
FROM Company INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) ON Company.CompanyId = BankStatementLine.CompanyId
WHERE (((CompanyId=CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_012-IZVOD-IZVODSTAVKE-POGRESNA-SZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatement.Date, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.PartnerAccountingId
FROM BankStatementLine INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId
WHERE (((BankStatement.CompanyId=BankStatementLine.CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_013-IZVODSTAVKE-GK-RAZLICIT-ID-K
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, Partner.CompanyId
FROM Partner INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) ON Partner.PartnerId = LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((PartnerAccountingId=PartnerAccountingId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_014-IZVOD-NALOG-SUMA-NIJE-NULA
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.BankStatementId, CompanyId & " - " & StatementNumber & " - " & BankStatement.Date AS Expr1, BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.Date, Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount,4)-Round(CreditAmount,4)),2) AS Sum
FROM (BankStatement INNER JOIN JournalEntry ON BankStatement.JournalEntryId = JournalEntry.JournalEntryNumber) INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON JournalEntry.JournalEntryNumber = LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
WHERE (((BankStatement.IsUnposted)=True))
GROUP BY BankStatement.BankStatementId, CompanyId & " - " & StatementNumber & " - " & BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.Date
HAVING (((Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount,4)-Round(CreditAmount,4)),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_014-IZVOD-UPOREDO_2410
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS BaseAmount, BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, BankStatement.PreviousBalance, BankStatement.NewBalance
FROM LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN BankStatement ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = BankStatement.JournalEntryId
WHERE (((BankStatement.Note) Is Null Or (BankStatement.Note)=""))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, BankStatement.PreviousBalance, BankStatement.NewBalance
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_015_GK_POVEZIVANJE_SUBKONTO_RAZLIKA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, PartnerAccountingId & " - " & DocumentRef AS info, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)='2040'))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, PartnerAccountingId & " - " & DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount
HAVING (((Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount))<>0) AND ((Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount))<>0) AND ((Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2))<0))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS info, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DueDate, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Is Null)) OR (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*") AND ((LedgerEntry.DueDate) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA_GROUP
-----SQL-----
SELECT ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.PartnerAccountingId, [ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA].PartnerAccountingId & " - " & BaseAmount AS INFO, ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.CompanyId, Count(ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.PartnerAccountingId) AS CountOflnkKUPACID
FROM ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA INNER JOIN GK_PARTNERI ON ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.PartnerAccountingId = GK_PARTNERI.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((BaseAmount>0)=-1))
GROUP BY [ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA].PartnerAccountingId & " - " & BaseAmount, ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.CompanyId, ERROR_016_GK_204X_DPO_DATUM_NEMA.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_017_GK_204X_POVEZIVANJE_SUBKONTA
-----SQL-----
SELECT Q.PartnerAccountingId, CompanyId & "-" &  PartnerId & " - " & Name AS ExchangeRateId
FROM (SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, Round(Sum(CreditAmount),2) AS SP, Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2) AS SD, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS BrojStavki
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like '204*'))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2))<>0) AND ((Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2))>-0.01))
)  AS Q INNER JOIN Partner ON Q.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY Q.PartnerAccountingId, CompanyId & "-" & PartnerId & " - " & Name
HAVING (((Sum(IIf([Q].[SPD]>0,1,0)))>0) AND ((Sum(IIf([Q].[SPD]<0,1,0)))>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_018_GK_PretplateIzvodi
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber & '-' & LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS Info, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, BankStatement.JournalEntryId, JournalEntryNumber=JournalEntryId AS Expr1, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)<0) AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=1) AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)>0) AND ((SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount)>0 Or (SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount) Is Null))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_019-IZVOD-2410-ODOBRENJE-ZADUZENJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT IzvodSumaZO.JournalEntryLineNumber, JournalEntryLineNumber & " - " & CompanyId AS Info, IzvodSumaZO.CompanyId, IzvodSumaZO.SumOfOdobrenje, IzvodSumaZO.SumOfZaduzenje, GK_2410_Sum.SumOfDIZNOS, GK_2410_Sum.SumOfPIZNOS, [SumOfOdobrenje]-[SumOfDIZNOS] AS Debit, [SumOfZaduzenje]-[SumOfPIZNOS] AS Credit
FROM IzvodSumaZO LEFT JOIN GK_2410_Sum ON IzvodSumaZO.JournalEntryLineNumber = GK_2410_Sum.JournalEntryNumber
WHERE ((([SumOfOdobrenje]-[SumOfDIZNOS])<>0)) OR ((([SumOfZaduzenje]-[SumOfPIZNOS])<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_020_OBJEKAT-SZ-KORISNIK-SZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, PartnerId & " / " & Partner.Name & "  -  " & CompanyId & " / " & [Skustina_1].Name & "  -  " & Unit.Name & " / " & CompanyId & " / " & Company.Name AS Expr1, Partner.Name, Skustina_1.Name, Unit.Name, Unit.CompanyId, Company.Name
FROM ((Partner INNER JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Company AS Skustina_1 ON Partner.CompanyId = Skustina_1.CompanyId
WHERE (((CompanyId=CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_021_RACUNI-DATUMPROMETA-DATUMVALUTE-RACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DueDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.Label, InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Date<>TransactionDate AS DP
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId = LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
WHERE (((Date<>TransactionDate)=-1));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_021_RACUNI-DATUMPROMETA-DATUMVALUTE-RACUNA-FIX
-----SQL-----
UPDATE [ERROR_021_RACUNI-TransactionDate-ValueDate-RACUNA] SET [ERROR_021_RACUNI-TransactionDate-ValueDate-RACUNA].Date = TransactionDate;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_022_MAILSEND_DONTHAVE_SENDDATE
-----SQL-----
SELECT SentEmail.*
FROM SentEmail
WHERE (((SentEmail.DateSent) Is Null) AND ((SentEmail.ErrorStatus) Is Null))
ORDER BY SentEmail.SentEmailId ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_023_GK4350_KONTOTROSKANULL
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((LedgerEntry.SubAccount) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.*, Invoice.IsCancelled
FROM LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_024_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN
-----SQL-----
SELECT ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN.InvoiceId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2) AS Debit, Round(Sum(CreditAmount),2) AS Credit
FROM ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN
WHERE (((ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN.IsCancelled)=True)) OR (((ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN.IsCancelled)=True))
GROUP BY ERROR_023_RACUN_STORO_PROKNJIZEN_PLACEN.InvoiceId
HAVING (((Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2))<>0)) OR (((Round(Sum(CreditAmount),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG
-----SQL-----
SELECT ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.JournalEntryNumber, [ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB].JournalEntryNumber & " / " & Note AS Expr1, Count(ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.CompanyId) AS CountOflnkSkupstinaID, Sum(ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.CountOfSTAVKAID) AS SumOfCountOfSTAVKAID, JournalEntry.Note
FROM ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB LEFT JOIN JournalEntry ON ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.JournalEntryNumber = JournalEntry.JournalEntryNumber
GROUP BY ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.JournalEntryNumber, [ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB].JournalEntryNumber & " / " & Note, JournalEntry.Note
HAVING (((Count(ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB.CompanyId))>1));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_027_GK_GRP_BY_NALOG_SUB
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_030_RACUNDOB-BEZGK-NEMA-SZ-ILI-NEMA-DOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId
FROM SupplierInvoice LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((SupplierInvoice.CompanyId) Is Null) AND ((LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) Is Null)) OR (((SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId) Is Null) AND ((LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_031_RACUNDOB-BEZGK-OBRISANA-SZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, Company.CompanyId
FROM (SupplierInvoice LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Company ON SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) Is Null) AND ((Company.CompanyId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_032_RACUNDOB-NIJE_KNJIZEN-NEMA-IDDOB-U-DOBAVLJACIMA
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, Partner.PartnerId
FROM (SupplierInvoice LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) Is Null) AND ((Partner.PartnerId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_033_RDOB_GRP
-----SQL-----
SELECT ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SupplierInvoiceId, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.Code, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.CompanyId, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.JournalEntryNumber, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.Date, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.CompanyId, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SumOfDIZNOS, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SumOfPIZNOS, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SubAccount, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.DocumentRef, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.InvoiceMonth, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SubAccount.Name, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.Name, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.KontrolaSK, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.PostingCount, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.Partner.Name, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.InvoiceAmountRsd, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.InvoiceName, ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.PostedInvoiceAmount
FROM ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP
WHERE (((ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SupplierInvoiceId) In (SELECT SupplierInvoiceId FROM [ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP] As Tmp GROUP BY SupplierInvoiceId HAVING Count(*)>1 )))
ORDER BY ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP.SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_033_SUB1_RDOB_GRP
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId AS KontrolaSK, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM ((((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)='2040' Or (LedgerEntry.Account)='4350'))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=CompanyId))
ORDER BY InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_041_RACUNDOB-GK-OBRISANA-SZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*, Company.CompanyId
FROM (SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Company ON SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Company.CompanyId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_042_RACUNDOB-GK-POGRRESAN-DOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.*, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, Partner.PartnerId
FROM (SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Partner.PartnerId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_043_GK_SIFRAKONTA_LNKKUPAC
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((PartnerAccountingId=AccountCode)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_060_RACUNI-BEZ-IDK-ILI-SZID-ILI-GRID
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.*, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId
FROM Invoice
WHERE (((Invoice.PartnerId)=0)) OR (((Invoice.CompanyId)=0)) OR (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_062_RACUNI_RACUNISTAVKE_GRID_RAZLICITO
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId AS Expr1
FROM InvoiceLine INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_063_RACUNI_RACUNISTAVKE_SZID_RAZLICITO
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.CompanyId=InvoiceLine.CompanyId AS Expr1, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId
FROM InvoiceLine INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((Invoice.CompanyId=InvoiceLine.CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_064_RACUNI_RACUNISTAVKE_IDK_RAZLICITO
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId, Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM InvoiceLine INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
GROUP BY Invoice.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId, Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Invoice.PartnerId=InvoiceLine.PartnerId
HAVING (((Invoice.PartnerId=InvoiceLine.PartnerId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_065_RACUNI_GK_SZID_RAZLICITO
-----SQL-----
SELECT CompanyId=CompanyId AS Expr1, Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.SequenceNumber, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((CompanyId=CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_066_RACUNI_GK_IDK_RAZLICITO
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerAccountingId=PartnerId AS Expr2, Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.SequenceNumber, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((PartnerAccountingId=PartnerId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_070_GR_NEMA_PODATAKA_ADELL
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
FROM Invoice RIGHT JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_070_GR_NEMA_PODATAKA_ADELL_EXECUTE
-----SQL-----
DELETE Invoice.InvoiceId, InvoiceBatch.*
FROM InvoiceBatch LEFT JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_071_RACUNI-RACUNISTAVKE-BEZ-RACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceLine.*
FROM Invoice RIGHT JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_071_RACUNI-RACUNISTAVKE-BEZ-RACUNA_EXECUTE
-----SQL-----
DELETE InvoiceLine.*, Invoice.InvoiceId
FROM Invoice RIGHT JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_075_GK-RDOB-GRPSK
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId)>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_081_RACUNI_SUME
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId, Count(Invoice.InvoiceId) AS CountOfIDRacun, Sum(Invoice.Total) AS SumOfUkupno
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=101));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_082_GK_2040
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_082_GK_4350
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.AccountCode
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.AccountCode
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_082_GK_4350_RDOB_KN
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_082_GK_4900
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4900"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_082_GK_5590
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="5590"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.Name, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId
FROM (InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.Name, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId
ORDER BY InvoiceBatch.IssueDate;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_084_UPOREDO-083-082
-----SQL-----
SELECT [ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC].*, ERROR_082_GK_4350.*, ERROR_082_GK_4900.*, ERROR_082_GK_5590.*, ERROR_082_GK_4350.AccountCode
FROM (([ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC] LEFT JOIN ERROR_082_GK_4350 ON [ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC].SupplierInvoiceId = ERROR_082_GK_4350.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN ERROR_082_GK_4900 ON [ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC].SupplierInvoiceId = ERROR_082_GK_4900.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN ERROR_082_GK_5590 ON [ERROR_083_GRRAC-STAVKE-DOBRAC].SupplierInvoiceId = ERROR_082_GK_5590.SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_085_SUME_GRUPARACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId, GK_2040.SumOfDIZNOS, GK_4350.SumOfPIZNOS, GK_4900.SumOfPIZNOS, GK_5590.SumOfDIZNOS
FROM (((GK_2040 RIGHT JOIN InvoiceBatch ON GK_2040.JournalEntryNumber = InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId) LEFT JOIN GK_4350 ON InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId = GK_4350.JournalEntryNumber) LEFT JOIN GK_5590 ON InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId = GK_5590.JournalEntryNumber) LEFT JOIN GK_4900 ON InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId = GK_4900.JournalEntryNumber;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_101_KONTOTROSKA_NEPOSTOJI
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, TKONTO.SubAccountCode
FROM LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN TKONTO ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode
WHERE ((Not (LedgerEntry.SubAccount) Is Null And (LedgerEntry.SubAccount)<>"") AND ((TKONTO.SubAccountCode) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_102_KONTOTROSKA_NEMAPARENT
-----SQL-----
SELECT TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.SubAccountCode, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode
FROM ((TKONTO LEFT JOIN SubAccount ON TKONTO.TKONTO = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.GRUPA1 = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_4 ON TKONTO.GRUPA4 = Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((SubAccount.SubAccountCode) Is Null)) OR (((Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) Is Null)) OR (((Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_103_2040_IZVOD_NEMASTAVKUIZVODA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)=0) AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=1) AND ((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040"));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_104_NALOG_RAVNOTEZA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Round(Sum(DebitAmount)-Sum(CreditAmount),2) AS BaseAmount
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
HAVING (((Round(Sum(DebitAmount)-Sum(CreditAmount),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA
-----SQL-----
SELECT Count(BankStatement.BankStatementId) AS CountOfIzvodID, BankStatement.JournalEntryId
FROM BankStatement
GROUP BY BankStatement.JournalEntryId
HAVING (((Count(BankStatement.BankStatementId))>1));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_901_AB_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA_GK_STAVKI
-----SQL-----
SELECT [ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA].JournalEntryId, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS GK_STAVKI
FROM LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber=[ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA].JournalEntryId
GROUP BY [ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA].JournalEntryId;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_901_AC_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA_IZVODSTAVKI
-----SQL-----
SELECT ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA.JournalEntryId, BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatement.CompanyId, Count(BankStatementLine.Id) AS CountOfID, "IZVOD " & StatementNumber & "/" & StatementSuffix AS Expr1
FROM BankStatementLine RIGHT JOIN (BankStatement RIGHT JOIN ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA ON BankStatement.JournalEntryId = ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA.JournalEntryId) ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId
GROUP BY ERROR_901_A_NALOG_VISE_IZVODA.JournalEntryId, BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatement.CompanyId, "IZVOD " & StatementNumber & "/" & StatementSuffix;


=====QUERY=====
ERROR_902_A_GK_STAVKA_BEZ_DATUMA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, "IzvodStavkaID :  / " & BankStatementLineId & " / RacunTroskaID :  " & InboundInvoiceId & " / PartnerID : " & PartnerAccountingId AS Expr1, LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
ERROR-IZVOD
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit) AS SumOfZaduzenje, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, JournalEntry.Description, JournalEntry.CompanyId, BankStatement.CompanyId
FROM ((BankStatement LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatement.JournalEntryId = LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber) INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) LEFT JOIN JournalEntry ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = JournalEntry.JournalEntryNumber
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, JournalEntry.Description, JournalEntry.CompanyId, BankStatement.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
ErrorIzvod-SumaNovStanjePrethodno-GK2410
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, NewBalance-PreviousBalance AS SumaPoIzvoduStanje, GK_2410_grpNalog.Sum, GK_2410_grpNalog.JournalEntryNumber, BankStatement.JournalEntryId, Round((NewBalance-PreviousBalance)-(Sum),2) AS RAZLIKA
FROM GK_2410_grpNalog LEFT JOIN BankStatement ON GK_2410_grpNalog.JournalEntryNumber = BankStatement.JournalEntryId
WHERE (((Round((NewBalance-PreviousBalance)-(Sum),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT_001_LISTA_OBJEKATA_SA_KORISNICIMA
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Name, Company.Building, BuildingEntrance.Entrance, BuildingEntrance.Address AS [Address ulaza], Unit.Name AS [Poseban deo], Partner.Name, Unit.K1 AS Kvadratura, UnitType.PrintLabel AS [Type dela], Partner.PartnerId AS SubAccountCode, Partner.Name AS UserName, Partner.TaxId
FROM Company INNER JOIN ((BuildingEntrance INNER JOIN (Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) ON BuildingEntrance.Entrance = Unit.Entrance) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Company.CompanyId = BuildingEntrance.CompanyId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=1))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT_002_LISTA_KORISNIKA_EMAIL
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Name, Company.Building, Partner.PartnerId AS SubAccountCode, PartnerEmail.Email, Partner.Name AS UserName
FROM ((PartnerEmail INNER JOIN Partner ON PartnerEmail.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Objekti_By_KupacID ON Partner.PartnerId = Objekti_By_KupacID.PartnerId
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail)=-1))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, PartnerEmail.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT_003_PREBROJAVANJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Name, Company.Building, UnitType.PrintLabel, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS BrojPosebnihDelova, Sum(Unit.K1) AS Kvadratura
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=1) AND ((Unit.PartnerId) Is Not Null))
GROUP BY Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.Building, UnitType.PrintLabel, UnitType.SortOrder
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT001 - Listing - SZ-PD-VL
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Company.Name AS [Stambena zajednica], Company.Building, Unit.Entrance, UnitType.PrintLabel AS [Vrsta posebnog dela], Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Partner.Name AS Vlasnik, Unit.K1 AS [K1 - m2], Unit.K2 AS [K2 - keoficijent], Unit.K3 AS [K3 - Number], Unit.K4 AS [K4 - Number]
FROM ((Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=1))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT002-ListingRacuna
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, Invoice.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.Building, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.Partner, Invoice.SequenceNumber, Invoice.InvoiceTotal, Invoice.InterestAmount, Invoice.Total, Invoice.PaymentReference
FROM (Invoice INNER JOIN Company ON Invoice.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=PeriodYyMm) AND ((Invoice.IsCancelled)=False))
ORDER BY Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT002-ListingRacuna-KONTROLA-003
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, First(Invoice.Sum) AS FirstOfSuma, Sum(InvoiceLine.Sum) AS SumOfSuma, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, Invoice.SequenceNumber, Round(First(Invoice.Sum)-Sum(InvoiceLine.Sum),2) AS Expr1
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN (Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceId) ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.SequenceNumber
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=PeriodYyMm) AND ((Round(First(Invoice.Sum)-Sum(InvoiceLine.Sum),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT003A-SumeStavkeRacunaKamata
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, SubAccount.Name, Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS
FROM ((PenaltyInterestStaging INNER JOIN (InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Company ON InvoiceBatch.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) ON PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Invoice ON (Invoice.PartnerId = PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId) AND (InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)
WHERE (((Invoice.IsCancelled)=False))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, SubAccount.Name, InvoiceBatch.Code
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=PeriodYyMm));


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT003-SumeStavkeRacuna
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, InvoiceLine.Name, Sum(InvoiceLine.Quantity) AS SumOfKolicina, InvoiceLine.UnitOfMeasure, InvoiceLine.PriceEur, InvoiceLine.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLine.Amount, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, InvoiceLine.SortOrder, Partner.Name
FROM (((Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Company ON InvoiceBatch.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, InvoiceLine.Name, InvoiceLine.UnitOfMeasure, InvoiceLine.PriceEur, InvoiceLine.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLine.Amount, InvoiceLine.SortOrder, Partner.Name, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Code
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=PeriodYyMm))
ORDER BY InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
Export004-SumeStavkeRacunaBenefiti
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.Name, Sum(InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.Quantity) AS SumOfKolicina, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.UnitOfMeasure, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.PriceEur, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.Amount, Sum(InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.SortOrder, Partner.Name
FROM (((Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLineBenefitArchive ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Company ON InvoiceBatch.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.Building, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.Name, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.UnitOfMeasure, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.PriceEur, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.Amount, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.SortOrder, Partner.Name, InvoiceBatch.Code
ORDER BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.CompanyId, InvoiceLineBenefitArchive.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT005_Benefiti_All
-----SQL-----
SELECT Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Company.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Company.Building, UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode AS Unit, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.K3, Partner.Name, Benefit.IsUsed
FROM Partner INNER JOIN (((Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Benefit ON Unit.UnitId = Benefit.UnitId) INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
ORDER BY Benefit.PeriodYyMm, Company.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT006_SUMA-RACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, Company.Building, Count(Invoice.InvoiceId) AS CountOfIDRacun, Sum(Partner.SkipPrintInvoiceGroup) AS SumOfchkSkipPrintRacunGrupa
FROM ((InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Company ON Invoice.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Invoice.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Invoice.IsCancelled)=False))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, Company.Building;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT007 - Listing - SZ-PD-VL - NEAKTIVNO
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Company.Name AS [Stambena zajednica], Company.Building, Unit.Entrance, UnitType.PrintLabel AS [Vrsta posebnog dela], Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Partner.Name AS Vlasnik, Unit.K1 AS [K1 - m2], Unit.K2 AS [K2 - keoficijent], Unit.K3 AS [K3 - Number]
FROM ((Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)<>1))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
EXPORT008-KontaktLista
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Company.Building, Partner.Name, Partner.Phone, Unit.Name, PartnerEmail.Email, PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail, UnitType.Name
FROM ((PartnerEmail RIGHT JOIN (Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) ON PartnerEmail.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) LEFT JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail)=-1 Or (PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail) Is Null))
ORDER BY Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, Max(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MaxOfDATUM, Min(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MinOfDATUM
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410"));


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00_PS_Y
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, Max(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MaxOfDATUM, Min(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MinOfDATUM, Year(Date) & ". /PROMET ZA PERIOD : " AS YYYYNASLOV
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Year(Date) & ". /PROMET ZA PERIOD : "
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410"));


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00_PSTS_Y
-----SQL-----
SELECT Account, CompanyId, SumOfDIZNOS, SumOfPIZNOS, Sum, MaxOfDATUM, MinOfDATUM, YYYYNASLOV
FROM Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00_PS_Y
UNION SELECT Account, CompanyId, SumOfDIZNOS, SumOfPIZNOS, Sum, MaxOfDATUM, MinOfDATUM, YYYYNASLOV
FROM Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00_TS
ORDER BY MinOfDATUM;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_00_TS
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, Max(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MaxOfDATUM, Min(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MinOfDATUM, "          . /PROMET ZA PERIOD : " AS YYYYNASLOV
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date) Between [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "          . /PROMET ZA PERIOD : "
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410"));

Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_01
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, Max(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MaxOfDATUM, Min(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MinOfDATUM

=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02
-----SQL-----
SELECT Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNaziv, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.Fakturisano4900, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Fakturisano2040, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Uplaceno2040, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PlacenoDob, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PotrazujuDob, Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]) AS Nenaplaceno2040
FROM ((Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0 INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.CompanyId) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.SubAccount)) INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.CompanyId) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.SubAccount)) INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.CompanyId) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.TKONTO)
WHERE (((Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1)<>"10"));


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_0
-----SQL-----
SELECT Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNaziv, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.Fakturisano4900, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Fakturisano2040, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Uplaceno2040, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PlacenoDob, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PotrazujuDob, [Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob] AS Balance, [Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob] AS Nenaplaceno2040
FROM ((Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0 INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.SubAccount) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub1.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.SubAccount) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4 ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.TKONTO) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.CompanyId)
WHERE (((Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1) Like "10*"));


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_2
-----SQL-----
SELECT Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa4, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Fakturisano2040) AS SumOfFakturisano2040, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.Uplaceno2040) AS SumOfUplaceno2040, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PlacenoDob) AS SumOfPlacenoDob, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.PotrazujuDob) AS SumOfPotrazujuDob, Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]) AS Balance, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.YYYY, Sum([Fakturisano2040]-[Uplaceno2040]) AS Nenaplaceno2040, IIf([grupa1]="10",Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]),IIf(Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob])<0,Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]),0)) AS FondStanje
FROM (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0 LEFT JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2 ON Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.JNEXP = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2.JNEXP) LEFT JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4 ON Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.JNEXP = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4.JNEXP
GROUP BY Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0.YYYY;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_2_PS_YY
-----SQL-----
SELECT Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.GRUPA4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKontoNazivGrupa4, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY.Fakturisano2040) AS SumOfFakturisano2040, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY.Uplaceno2040) AS SumOfUplaceno2040, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY.PlacenoDob) AS SumOfPlacenoDob, Sum(Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY.PotrazujuDob) AS SumOfPotrazujuDob, Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]) AS Balance, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY, Sum([Fakturisano2040]-[Uplaceno2040]) AS Nenaplaceno2040, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY AS ShowYY, IIf([grupa1]="10",Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]),IIf(Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob])<0,Sum([Uplaceno2040]-[PlacenoDob]),0)) AS FondStanje
FROM (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY.YYYY) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY.SubAccount) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub2_PS_YY.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY ON (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY.YYYY) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKONTO = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY.TKONTO) AND (Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.CompanyId = Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4_PS_YY.CompanyId)
GROUP BY Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.CompanyId, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.GRUPA4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKontoNazivGrupa4, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.GRUPA1, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.TKontoNazivGrupa, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY, Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY
ORDER BY Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY.YYYY;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_2_PSTS_YY
-----SQL-----
SELECT CompanyId, GRUPA4, TKontoNazivGrupa4, SumOfFakturisano2040, SumOfUplaceno2040, SumOfPlacenoDob, SumOfPotrazujuDob, Balance, GRUPA1, TKontoNazivGrupa, Nenaplaceno2040 , YYYY, YYYY AS SHOWYY, FondStanje
FROM Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_2_PS_YY
UNION SELECT CompanyId, GRUPA4, TKontoNazivGrupa4, SumOfFakturisano2040, SumOfUplaceno2040, SumOfPlacenoDob, SumOfPotrazujuDob, Balance, GRUPA1, TKontoNazivGrupa, Nenaplaceno2040, YYYY, "" AS SHOWYY, FondStanje
FROM Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_2
ORDER BY YYYY;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0
-----SQL-----
SELECT frmIzvestajFilterGK.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name AS TKontoNaziv, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa4, Year(Max(Date)) AS YYYY, CompanyId & "-" & SubAccount AS JNEXP
FROM ((frmIzvestajFilterGK INNER JOIN (TKONTO INNER JOIN SubAccount ON TKONTO.TKONTO = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON frmIzvestajFilterGK.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.GRUPA1 = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_4 ON TKONTO.GRUPA4 = Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((frmIzvestajFilterGK.Date) Between [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]) AND ((frmIzvestajFilterGK.SubAccount) Like "1*"))
GROUP BY frmIzvestajFilterGK.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name, CompanyId & "-" & SubAccount;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name AS TKontoNaziv, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa4
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN (TKONTO INNER JOIN SubAccount ON TKONTO.TKONTO = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.GRUPA1 = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_4 ON TKONTO.GRUPA4 = Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd]) AND ((LedgerEntry.SubAccount) Like "1*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub0_PS_YY
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name AS TKontoNaziv, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa4, Year(Date) AS YYYY
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN (TKONTO INNER JOIN SubAccount ON TKONTO.TKONTO = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.GRUPA1 = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_4 ON TKONTO.GRUPA4 = Troskovi_PodKonta_4.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd]) AND ((LedgerEntry.SubAccount) Like "1*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, TKONTO.TKONTO, SubAccount.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_4.Name, Year(Date)
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]))
ORDER BY Year(Date);

=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub4

=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_02_sub5
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Paid, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, "" AS YYYY
FROM (((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN TKONTO ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350" Or (LedgerEntry.Account)="5532") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, Partner.PartnerId;

Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_03
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name AS SupplierName, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNaziv, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS SubAccountName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0) AS [Debt-TMP], IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)=0,Sum(DebitAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS [OBRACUNATO-TMP], IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532',0,IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0)) AS Debt, IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532' Or LedgerEntry.Account='5241',Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS OBRACUNATO
FROM (((SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_2 INNER JOIN ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN TKONTO ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON Troskovi_PodKonta_2.SubAccountCode = TKONTO.GRUPA1) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.TKONTO = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]) AND ((TKONTO.CostAllocationTarget) Like "10*")) OR (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]) AND ((TKONTO.CostAllocationTarget) Like "10*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350" Or (LedgerEntry.Account)="5532") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId) Is Null)) OR (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId)=2))
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate;


=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_03_1
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name AS SupplierName, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNaziv, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS SubAccountName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0) AS [Debt-TMP], IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)=0,Sum(DebitAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS [OBRACUNATO-TMP], IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532',0,IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0)) AS Debt, IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532' Or LedgerEntry.Account='5241',Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS OBRACUNATO, InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker
FROM (((((SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_2 INNER JOIN ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN TKONTO ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON Troskovi_PodKonta_2.SubAccountCode = TKONTO.GRUPA1) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.TKONTO = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040") AND (Not (InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Is Null And (InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Not Like "V*" And (InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Not Like ""))
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate;

WHERE (((frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account)="5532") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId) Is Null)) OR (((frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account)="4350") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId)=2)) OR (((frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account)="4350") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId) Is Null)) OR (((frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account)="5241") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId) Is Null))

Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_03_PS
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name AS SupplierName, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name AS TKontoNazivGrupa, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name AS TKontoNaziv, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS SubAccountName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Nz(SupplierInvoiceId,0)=0,Max(LedgerEntry.Date),SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate) AS DatumRacunaMix, IIf(Nz(SupplierInvoiceId,0)=0,LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId,"") AS Grupisanje, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)=0 AS Zatvoreno
FROM (((SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_2 INNER JOIN ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN TKONTO ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) ON Troskovi_PodKonta_2.SubAccountCode = TKONTO.GRUPA1) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.TKONTO = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd]) AND ((TKONTO.CostAllocationTarget) Like "10*")) OR (((LedgerEntry.Date)<[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd]) AND ((TKONTO.CostAllocationTarget) Like "10*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Nz(SupplierInvoiceId,0)=0,LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId,"")
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="5532" Or (LedgerEntry.Account)="4350" Or (LedgerEntry.Account)="5241") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId) Is Null)) OR (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId)=2))
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate;

GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name, TKONTO.GRUPA1, Troskovi_PodKonta_2.Name, TKONTO.TKONTO, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Nz(SupplierInvoiceId,0)=0,LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId,"")
```
