=====QUERY=====
Fin_Izvestaj_SZ_06_2_SUB1
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, Partner.Name AS SupplierName, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS SubAccountName, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate, IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0) AS [Debt-TMP], IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)=0,Sum(DebitAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS [OBRACUNATO-TMP], IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532',0,IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)>0,Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount),0)) AS Debt, IIf(LedgerEntry.Account='5532' Or LedgerEntry.Account='5241',Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),Sum(CreditAmount)) AS OBRACUNATO, SubAccount.SubAccountCode, LedgerEntry.Date
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))

HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId)=3))
ORDER BY SupplierInvoice.InvoiceDate;

-----SQL-----

=====QUERY=====
FIX_NULLS
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Invoice SET Invoice.PreviousDebt = 0
WHERE (((Invoice.PreviousDebt) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
Fix_Rdob_Table5
-----SQL-----
UPDATE LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Table5 ON LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId = Table5.LedgerEntryId SET LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = [NewRdob];


=====QUERY=====
Fix_RFob
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoiceId=SupplierInvoiceId AS Expr1, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.TotalRsd, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId
FROM (InvoiceLine INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON (InvoiceLine.PartnerId = LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId) AND (InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = LedgerEntry.InvoiceId) AND (SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount = LedgerEntry.SubAccount) AND (LedgerEntry.CreditAmount = InvoiceLine.TotalRsd)
WHERE (((SupplierInvoiceId=SupplierInvoiceId)=0) AND ((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=129));


=====QUERY=====
Fix_SortRacun
-----SQL-----
UPDATE (InvoiceLine INNER JOIN Unit ON InvoiceLine.UnitId = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId SET InvoiceLine.SortOrder = UnitType.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
FIX_TEMP_KUPAC_PDBD
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Partner SET Partner.PBPD_F = Replace(Partner.PBPD,"-","")
WHERE (((Partner.PBPD) Is Not Null));


=====QUERY=====
FIX001_Izvod_Rasknjizenje
-----SQL-----
UPDATE (BankStatementLine LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId SET BankStatement.Note = Null
WHERE (((BankStatement.Note)="R") AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
FIX002_RACUN_CUSTOM-NAPOMENA
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Invoice SET Invoice.AdditionalNote = [UNESITE TEKST NAPOMENE]
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=[UNESITE Id GRUPE RACUNA]) AND ((Invoice.CompanyId)=[UNESITE Id SKUPSTINE]));


=====QUERY=====
FIX003_ERROR_010-STAVKE-GK-DOBAVLJACI-BEZ-SK
-----SQL-----
UPDATE (Company INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN (BankStatementLine INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) ON Company.CompanyId = BankStatement.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId SET LedgerEntry.CompanyId = BankStatement.CompanyId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
fltFormDobavljaci
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, Count(SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) AS BRP
FROM SupplierInvoice
WHERE (((SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth)=Forms!GrupaRacuna_Add!txtYYMM Or (SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth) Is Null) And ((SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId)=1))
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
fltFormRacuni
-----SQL-----
SELECT Count(Invoice.InvoiceId) AS BRR, Invoice.CompanyId
FROM Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=Forms!GrupaRacuna_Add!txtYYMM Or (InvoiceBatch.Code) Is Null) And ((InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Is Null))
GROUP BY Invoice.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
fltFormRacuniVR
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, Count(InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) AS CountOfMarkerVanderdnihRacuna
FROM InvoiceBatch
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=Forms!GrupaRacuna_Add!txtYYMM Or (InvoiceBatch.Code) Is Null) And ((InvoiceBatch.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Is Not Null))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.CompanyId;

fltMarkerList

FROM CodeList
WHERE (((CodeList.Category)="MarkerRacuna"));


=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_Dobavljaci
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Sum(frmIzvestajFilterGK.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(frmIzvestajFilterGK.CreditAmount) AS Potražuje, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Balance, First(frmIzvestajFilterGK.CompanyId) AS FirstOflnkSkupstinaID
FROM frmIzvestajFilterGK RIGHT JOIN Partner ON frmIzvestajFilterGK.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Partner.Account)="4350" Or (Partner.Account)="5532"))
GROUP BY Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, frmIzvestajFilterGK.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((First(frmIzvestajFilterGK.CompanyId))=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));

frmIzvestaj_GK_Filter_001
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)>=#1/1/2019# And (LedgerEntry.Date)<=#3/16/2019#) AND ((LedgerEntry.Account) Like '2040*') AND ((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=101));


=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_GK_Filter_001_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(frmIzvestaj_GK_Filter_001.CreditAmount) AS Debt

=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_Izvod
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix AS FiscalYear, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS KONTOPARTNERA, Partner.Name AS NazivPartnera, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS NazivTroska, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId
FROM ((((BankStatement INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
GROUP BY BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, BankStatementLine.LineNumber
HAVING (((BankStatement.CompanyId)=Forms!Izvestaji!cmbSZ) And ((BankStatement.Date)>=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumOd And (BankStatement.Date)<=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumDo))
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date, BankStatementLine.LineNumber;


=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_Izvod_grp
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix AS FiscalYear, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS KONTOPARTNERA, Partner.Name AS NazivPartnera, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit
FROM (((BankStatement INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, BankStatement.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit
HAVING (((BankStatement.CompanyId)=Forms!Izvestaji!cmbSZ) And ((BankStatement.Date)>=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumOd And (BankStatement.Date)<=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumDo))
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date, BankStatementLine.LineNumber;

frmIzvestaj_Objekti
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name AS UserName, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.Name AS Prostor, Partner.CompanyId, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.K1, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.K2, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.K3, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.K4, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.K5, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.SortOrder, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.UnitTypeCode, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.Name, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.UnitId, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.Entrance, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.UnitTypeShortCode, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.Status
FROM Partner INNER JOIN ObjektiSaTipomKoef ON Partner.PartnerId = ObjektiSaTipomKoef.PartnerId
WHERE (((Partner.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]))
ORDER BY ObjektiSaTipomKoef.SortOrder, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.UnitTypeCode, ObjektiSaTipomKoef.Name;


=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_Stanari
-----SQL-----

FROM ObjektiSaTipom RIGHT JOIN Partner ON ObjektiSaTipom.UnitId = Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode
WHERE (((Partner.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]))
GROUP BY Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Partner.CompanyId, SpojRedove("SifraPD","Objekti","lnk_ID_K=" & PartnerId,"BrojPD")

=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_StanariBrCl
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitId, Unit.Name AS [Name posebnog dela], Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name AS UserName, Unit.ResidentCount AS [Number clanova domacinstva], Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.K3, Unit.K4, Unit.K5
FROM (Partner INNER JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Unit.ResidentCount)>0) And ((Unit.CompanyId)=Forms!Izvestaji!cmbSZ))
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Unit.Name;


=====QUERY=====
frmIzvestaj_StanariStanje
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Sum(frmIzvestajFilterGK.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(frmIzvestajFilterGK.CreditAmount) AS Potražuje, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Balance, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode AS Prostor, First(Partner.CompanyId) AS FirstOflnk_ID_SK, IIf(Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)>0,Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2),0) AS Debit, IIf(Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)<0,-Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2),0) AS Credit, Unit.UnitTypeCode, IIf(Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)<-0.001,-1,IIf(Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)>-0.001 And Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2)<0.001,0,IIf(Sum(CreditAmount)=0,2,1))) AS Grupa, IIf(GroupInvoiceTagId=0,-1,GroupInvoiceTagId=PartnerId) AS FilterKupac, Nz([Total],0) AS UnpaidInvoiceCount
FROM ((frmIzvestajFilterGK RIGHT JOIN Partner ON frmIzvestajFilterGK.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) LEFT JOIN GK_GRP_DOK_COUNT_BRMD ON Partner.PartnerId = GK_GRP_DOK_COUNT_BRMD.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((Partner.Account)="2040"))
GROUP BY Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.UnitTypeCode, IIf(GroupInvoiceTagId=0,-1,GroupInvoiceTagId=PartnerId), frmIzvestajFilterGK.PartnerAccountingId, Nz([Total],0)
HAVING (((First(Partner.CompanyId))=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]) AND ((IIf(GroupInvoiceTagId=0,-1,GroupInvoiceTagId=PartnerId))=-1))
ORDER BY Unit.UnitTypeCode;

=====QUERY=====
GEN_R_001
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( Objekat, InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, UnitId, CompanyId, Quantity, UnitOfMeasure, JO )
SELECT Unit.Name, 1 AS Expr3, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.UnitId, Unit.CompanyId, Unit.Quantity, Unit.UnitOfMeasure, Unit.JO
FROM Unit LEFT JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId=Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=0));


=====QUERY=====
GEN_R_002
-----SQL-----
UPDATE InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId SET InvoiceLine.ExchangeRateNbs = InvoiceBatch.ExchangeRateNbs, InvoiceLine.Price = InvoiceLine.Quantity*InvoiceLine.JO, InvoiceLine.Total = Round(InvoiceLine.Quantity*InvoiceLine.JO*InvoiceBatch.ExchangeRateNbs,2)
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=1));


=====QUERY=====
GEN_R_003
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( PartnerId, CompanyId, Total, PageCount, IssueDate, PlaceOfIssue, ServiceDate, Partner, PostalCode, Address, TaxId, StanjePredhodniDug, StanjeDug, Currency, ObavestanjePDug, PozivNaBrojPDug, ValueDate, PaymentPurpose, PaymentReference, InvoiceBatchId )
SELECT InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, Sum(InvoiceLine.Total) AS SumOfUkupno, Count(InvoiceLine.InvoiceLineId) AS CountOfIDRacunStavke, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, StanjePP.Balance, StanjeSveBezPP.Balance, "RSD" AS Expr1, "*Molimo Vas da izmirite dugovanje koje se odnosi na period do 31.03.2012. uplatom na iskazani tekuci racun sa pozivom na broj  " AS Expr2, StanjePP.FirstOfNAPOMENA, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, "RACUN ZA ODRŽAVANJE STAMBENOG NASELJA BELVILLE - " & InvoiceBatch.Label AS Expr3, KontrolniBroj(97,Code & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.CompanyId,'00') & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000')) & "-" & Code & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.CompanyId,'00') & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000') AS ReferenceCode, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId
FROM StanjeSveBezPP RIGHT JOIN (((InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Partner ON InvoiceLine.PartnerId=Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN StanjePP ON (InvoiceLine.CompanyId=StanjePP.CompanyId) AND (InvoiceLine.PartnerId=StanjePP.PartnerAccountingId)) ON (StanjeSveBezPP.CompanyId=InvoiceLine.CompanyId) AND (StanjeSveBezPP.PartnerAccountingId=InvoiceLine.PartnerId)
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=1))
GROUP BY InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, StanjePP.Balance, StanjeSveBezPP.Balance, "RSD", "*Molimo Vas da izmirite dugovanje koje se odnosi na period do 31.03.2012. uplatom na iskazani tekuci racun sa pozivom na broj  ", StanjePP.FirstOfNAPOMENA, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, "RACUN ZA ODRŽAVANJE STAMBENOG NASELJA BELVILLE - " & InvoiceBatch.Label, KontrolniBroj(97,Code & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.CompanyId,'00') & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000')) & "-" & Code & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.CompanyId,'00') & "-" & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000'), InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId;

GEN_R_020
-----SQL-----

GK_Filter_PD
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*, IIf(PostingTypeCode>98 Or PostingTypeCode<95,0,1)+IIf(LineTypeId>98 Or LineTypeId<95,0,1) AS Flt, IIf(IIf(PostingTypeCode>LineTypeId,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId)<90,0,IIf(PostingTypeCode>LineTypeId,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId)) AS GTS

=====QUERY=====
GK_Filter_PD_Opomene
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*, IIf(PostingTypeCode>98 Or PostingTypeCode<95,0,1)+IIf(LineTypeId>98 Or LineTypeId<95,0,1) AS Flt, IIf(IIf(PostingTypeCode>LineTypeId,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId)<90,0,IIf(PostingTypeCode>LineTypeId,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId)) AS GTS
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE ((((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)<>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=#9/12/2026#)) OR (((LedgerEntry.DebitAmount)<=0) AND ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=#9/12/2026#))) OR (((LedgerEntry.DebitAmount)<>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=#7/31/2026#));

=====QUERY=====
GK_GKTMP_UNION
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.Note, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.Description, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.DueDate, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Priority, 0 as FromLedgerEntryId
FROM LedgerEntry

UNION SELECT LedgerEntryTemp.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntryTemp.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntryTemp.Account, LedgerEntryTemp.Date, LedgerEntryTemp.CreditAmount, LedgerEntryTemp.DebitAmount, LedgerEntryTemp.LineTypeId, LedgerEntryTemp.DocumentRef, LedgerEntryTemp.CompanyId, LedgerEntryTemp.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntryTemp.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntryTemp.Note, LedgerEntryTemp.Parameters, LedgerEntryTemp.Description, LedgerEntryTemp.AccountCode, LedgerEntryTemp.DueDate, LedgerEntryTemp.PostingCode, LedgerEntryTemp.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntryTemp.InvoiceId, LedgerEntryTemp.Priority, FromLedgerEntryId 
FROM LedgerEntryTemp;


=====QUERY=====
GK_Grp_Dok
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, IIf(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount)>0,1,0) AS IsDug, IIf(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount)<0,1,0) AS IsNotDug
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)='2040') And ((Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount-CreditAmount))>1 Or (Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount-CreditAmount))<-1));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_COUNT
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, Company.Name, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
FROM (GK_Grp_Dok INNER JOIN Company ON GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, Company.Name, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
HAVING (((GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId)=Forms!Izvestaji!cmbSZ) And ((Sum(GK_Grp_Dok.IsDug))>0) And ((Sum(GK_Grp_Dok.IsNotDug))>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_COUNT_ALL
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, Company.Name, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
FROM (GK_Grp_Dok INNER JOIN Company ON GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, Company.Name, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
HAVING (((Sum(GK_Grp_Dok.IsDug))>0) AND ((Sum(GK_Grp_Dok.IsNotDug))>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_COUNT_BRMD
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, GK_Grp_Dok.Account, Count(GK_Grp_Dok.InvoiceId) AS Total
FROM GK_Grp_Dok
WHERE (((GK_Grp_Dok.IsDug)=1) AND ((GK_Grp_Dok.IsNotDug)=0))
GROUP BY GK_Grp_Dok.CompanyId, GK_Grp_Dok.PartnerAccountingId, GK_Grp_Dok.Account;


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_DOB_ALL
-----SQL-----
SELECT First(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS FirstOfSTAVKAID, First(LedgerEntry.Date) AS FirstOfDATUM, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))>0,1,0) AS IsDug, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))<0,1,0) AS IsNotDug
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_DOB_ALL_izvod
-----SQL-----
SELECT First(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS FirstOfSTAVKAID, First(LedgerEntry.Date) AS FirstOfDATUM, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))>0,1,0) AS IsDug, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))<0,1,0) AS IsNotDug, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350") AND ((Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL
-----SQL-----
SELECT First(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS FirstOfSTAVKAID, First(LedgerEntry.Date) AS FirstOfDATUM, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))>0,1,0) AS IsDug, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))<0,1,0) AS IsNotDug, LedgerEntry.SubAccount
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like '204*') AND ((Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL_LISTING
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.CompanyId, GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.Sum) AS SumOfSuma, Sum(GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.IsDug) AS SumOfIsDug, Sum(GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.IsNotDug) AS SumOfIsNotDug
FROM GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL
GROUP BY GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.CompanyId, GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.CompanyId)=129) AND ((Sum(GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.IsDug))>0) AND ((Sum(GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL.IsNotDug))>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_GRP_DOK_KORISNIK_ALL_tmpKT
-----SQL-----
SELECT First(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS FirstOfSTAVKAID, First(LedgerEntry.Date) AS FirstOfDATUM, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))>0,1,0) AS IsDug, IIf(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2))<0,1,0) AS IsNotDug
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)='2040') AND ((Round(Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_Konta_Sum
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS BaseAmount, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN TKONTO ON SubAccount.SubAccountCode = TKONTO.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN SubAccount AS Troskovi_PodKonta_1 ON TKONTO.GRUPA4 = Troskovi_PodKonta_1.SubAccountCode
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Account, TKONTO.GRUPA4, Troskovi_PodKonta_1.Name;


=====QUERY=====
GK_Konta_Sum_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(GK_Konta_Sum.BaseAmount) AS SumOfDIPI
SELECT GK_Konta_Sum.SubAccount, GK_Konta_Sum.Name, Sum(GK_Konta_Sum.BaseAmount) AS [Total Of BaseAmount]
FROM GK_Konta_Sum
GROUP BY GK_Konta_Sum.SubAccount, GK_Konta_Sum.Name
PIVOT GK_Konta_Sum.Account;


=====QUERY=====
GK_KORISNIK_RDOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.Priority, LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040"));


=====QUERY=====
GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_UPOREDO
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.JournalEntryNumber, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.LedgerEntryId, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.CompanyId, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.PartnerAccountingId, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.DebitAmount, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.CreditAmount, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.SupplierInvoiceId AS SETRSDOB, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.SupplierInvoiceId, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.Priority AS SETRSPRIORITET, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.Priority, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.DocumentRef, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.DocumentRef, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.Parameters, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.Parameters, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.AccountCode, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.AccountCode, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.PostingCode, GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.PostingCode
FROM GK_KORISNIK_RDOB INNER JOIN GK_KORISNIK_RDOB AS GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1 ON (GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.InvoiceId = GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.InvoiceId) AND (GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.SubAccount = GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.SubAccount) AND (GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.DebitAmount = GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.CreditAmount) AND (GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.PartnerAccountingId = GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.PartnerAccountingId) AND (GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.CompanyId = GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.CompanyId)
WHERE (((GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.SupplierInvoiceId)<>0) AND ((GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.SupplierInvoiceId)<>0) AND ((GK_KORISNIK_RDOB.BankStatementLineId)=0) AND ((GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1.BankStatementLineId)<>0) AND (([GK_KORISNIK_RDOB].SupplierInvoiceId=[GK_KORISNIK_RDOB_1].SupplierInvoiceId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Date
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
HAVING (((Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount))>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)=0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG_SUB.PartnerAccountingId AS Expr1, Min(GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG_SUB.Date) AS MinOfDATUM
FROM GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG_SUB
GROUP BY GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG_SUB.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNER_NAJSTARIJI_DUG_SUB
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS BaseAmount, GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA.Date
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA ON (LedgerEntry.DocumentRef = GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA.DocumentRef) AND (LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA.PartnerAccountingId)
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, GK_PARTNER_DOK_DATUM_RACUNA.Date
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId)>0) AND ((Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount))>0.01));


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNER_POSLEDNJA_UPLATA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Max(LedgerEntry.Date) AS MaxOfDATUM
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)>0))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNER_SUM_PRINTERBIN
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_PARTNERI.PartnerAccountingId AS ExchangeRateId, [SumOfDIZNOS]-[SumOfPIZNOS] AS BaseAmount
FROM SelectionBasket INNER JOIN GK_PARTNERI ON SelectionBasket.TargetId = GK_PARTNERI.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
GK_PARTNERI
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS BaseAmount
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
GK_PIZNOS
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_Po_Izvodu
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, LedgerEntry.Date, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS BrojUlazaUGK
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.Date
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)>0));


=====QUERY=====
GK_POCETNOSTANJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS BaseAmount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Min(LedgerEntry.Priority) AS MinOfPRIORITET, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, Nz(AccountingDocumentId,0) AS KnDokID_NZ
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, Nz(AccountingDocumentId,0)
HAVING (((Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2))<>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=129));


=====QUERY=====
GK_PRETPLATEIZVODI_GRUPBY_KUPACNALOG
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_PretplateIzvodi.PartnerAccountingId, Count(GK_PretplateIzvodi.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID, GK_PretplateIzvodi.JournalEntryNumber
FROM GK_PretplateIzvodi
GROUP BY GK_PretplateIzvodi.PartnerAccountingId, GK_PretplateIzvodi.JournalEntryNumber;


=====QUERY=====
gk_R_201207
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, Left(Parameters,5) & "8" & Right(Parameters,Len(Parameters)-6) AS Expr1
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber)=4) AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=3) AND ((LedgerEntry.Parameters) Is Not Null));


=====QUERY=====
GK_RDOB_4350
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="4350"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
Gk_SumByIzvodStavke
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId;


=====QUERY=====
GK-BYMESEC
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Name, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Left(Parameters,4) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,5,2) AS Month, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, IIf(Sum(DebitAmount)>0,"R","U") AS RP
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Partner.DOB)=0))
GROUP BY Company.Name, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Left(Parameters,4) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,5,2), LedgerEntry.LineTypeId
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Left(Parameters,4) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,5,2), IIf(Sum(DebitAmount)>0,"R","U") ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
GK-BYMESEC_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum([LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].Amount) AS SumOfIznos
SELECT [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.Name, [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].PartnerAccountingId
FROM ([LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP] INNER JOIN Company ON [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Partner ON [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.Name, [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].PartnerAccountingId
ORDER BY [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].CompanyId, [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].PartnerAccountingId, [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].ZA
PIVOT [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC-RP].ZA;


=====QUERY=====
GK-BYMESEC-RP
-----SQL-----
SELECT [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC].*, Month & "-" & [RP] AS ZA, IIf([SumOfDIZNOS]>0,[SumOfDIZNOS],[SumOfPIZNOS]) AS Amount
FROM [LedgerEntry-BYMESEC];


=====QUERY=====
GK-R2305
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.Note, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.Description, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.DueDate, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, 77 AS RDB, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Priority, LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.AccountingDocumentId
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.DocumentRef)="R-2305") AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=3) AND ((LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId)=79 Or (LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId)=81))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, LedgerEntry.Note, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.Description, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.DueDate, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.Priority, LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.AccountingDocumentId;


=====QUERY=====
GK-R2306
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=3) AND ((LedgerEntry.DocumentRef)="R-2306"));


=====QUERY=====
GrupaOpomenaSaGrupomRacuna
-----SQL-----
SELECT ReminderBatch.ReminderBatchId, ReminderBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Code
FROM ReminderBatch INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON ReminderBatch.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId;


=====QUERY=====
GrupByStavke
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, Sum(InvoiceLine.Total) AS SumOfUkupno, InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeDescription
FROM (InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN ExpenseTypeSubtype ON InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.CalculationType) Is Not Null))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeDescription;


=====QUERY=====
GrupByStavke_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(GrupByStavke.SumOfUkupno) AS SumOfSumOfUkupno
SELECT GrupByStavke.ExpenseTypeDescription, Sum(GrupByStavke.SumOfUkupno) AS [Total Of SumOfUkupno]
FROM GrupByStavke
GROUP BY GrupByStavke.ExpenseTypeDescription
PIVOT GrupByStavke.Code;


=====QUERY=====
GrupisanjeKonta
-----SQL-----
SELECT Mid(Parameters,3,6) AS MES, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount*[IG_V]) AS [IG-DIPIP], Sum(DebitAmount*[CB_V]) AS [CB-DIPIP], Unit.UnitTypeId, UnitType.IgAccount, UnitType.CbAccount, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount) & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount AS ReferenceTag, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount) & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount AS [ReferenceTag-CB], LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "IF" & KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & "-" & IgAccount AS ifpar, "IF" & KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & "-" & CbAccount AS ifparCB
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Unit.PartnerId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Partner.Name)="Imperial Gradnja d.o.o") AND ((LedgerEntry.Note) Is Null))
GROUP BY Mid(Parameters,3,6), LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, Unit.UnitTypeId, UnitType.IgAccount, UnitType.CbAccount, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount) & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount) & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "IF" & KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & IgAccount) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & "-" & IgAccount, "IF" & KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(Parameters,3,6) & CbAccount) & "-" & Mid(Parameters,3,6) & "-" & CbAccount;


=====QUERY=====
GrupisanjeKonta_Storno
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, DebitAmount, Date, LineTypeId, CompanyId, Note, Account, DocumentRef, PartnerAccountingId, Parameters )
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, -DebitAmount AS Expr1, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "SPC" AS Expr2, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Parameters
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Partner.Name)="Imperial Gradnja d.o.o") AND ((LedgerEntry.Note) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
GrupisanjeKontaAdd
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, LineTypeId, DebitAmount, DocumentRef, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, Parameters, Note )
SELECT GrupisanjeKonta.JournalEntryNumber, 2020 & [IG-DIPIP] AS Expr1, GrupisanjeKonta.Date, GrupisanjeKonta.LineTypeId, GrupisanjeKonta.[IG-DIPIP], GrupisanjeKonta.ifpar, GrupisanjeKonta.CompanyId, GrupisanjeKonta.IgAccount, GrupisanjeKonta.ReferenceTag, "ZBIRNI" AS Expr2
FROM GrupisanjeKonta;


=====QUERY=====
GrupisanjeKontaAddCB
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, LineTypeId, DebitAmount, DocumentRef, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, Parameters, Note )
SELECT GrupisanjeKonta.JournalEntryNumber, 2020 & [CB-DIPIP] AS Expr1, GrupisanjeKonta.Date, GrupisanjeKonta.LineTypeId, GrupisanjeKonta.[CB-DIPIP], GrupisanjeKonta.ifparCB, GrupisanjeKonta.CompanyId, GrupisanjeKonta.CbAccount, GrupisanjeKonta.[ReferenceTag-CB], "ZBIRNI" AS Expr2
FROM GrupisanjeKonta;


=====QUERY=====
GrupIzvodBySK
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, Count(BankStatement.BankStatementId) AS CountOfIzvodID, Company.Name, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber
FROM BankStatement INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber;


=====QUERY=====
INFO-GENREC-SQL0
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, UnitId, CompanyId, Area, [MAX-CN], [MAX-Area], [MAX-Sum], [MAX-KOEF], KOEF, Bold, CalculationType, Name, Name, SortOrder, Text, ExpenseGroup, VP, ExpenseTypeId, DobAlias, [DSP-Sum], [MAX-Area-SVE], [MAX-GarageSpotNumber], [MAX-Area-K1], [MAX-Area-K2], [MAX-Area-K3] )
SELECT 133 AS GRPRC, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.UnitId, Unit.CompanyId, Unit.Quantity, Objekat_Sume.[MAX-CN], Objekat_Sume.[MAX-Area], Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.Value, Objekat_Sume.[MAX-KO], Unit.Coefficient, Len(Name)=1 AS IsBoldFont, Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.CalculationType, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name & '  ' & ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeDescription AS Expr1, ExpenseTypeSubtype.SortOrder, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Text, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseGroup, IIf(IsNumeric(Name),Name,'') AS Expr2, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId, Partner.Alias, Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.SumaD, Objekat_Sume_Sve.[MAX-Area-SVE], Objekat_Sume_Sve.[MAX-GarageSpotNumber], Objekat_Sume_Sve.[MAX-Area-K1], Objekat_Sume_Sve.[MAX-Area-K2], Objekat_Sume_Sve.[MAX-Area-K3]
FROM ((Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT RIGHT JOIN (Objekat_Sume INNER JOIN ((Unit INNER JOIN ExpenseTypeSubtype ON Unit.UnitTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.UnitTypeReportLabel) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Objekat_Sume.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.ExpenseTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.SupplierPartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Objekat_Sume_Sve ON Unit.CompanyId=Objekat_Sume_Sve.CompanyId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=0))
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name;


=====QUERY=====
izv_kratica_zbirna_kupca
-----SQL-----
SELECT Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '' AS Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef AS DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, First(LedgerEntry.InvoiceId) AS FirstOfRACID, First(Invoice.TransactionDate) AS FirstOfDatumPrometa, Company.Name, Partner.Name, Company.PrintName, Company.InvoicePostalCode, Company.InvoiceCity, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber, InvoiceBatch.Label
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*") And ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between Forms!Kartica!txtDatumOd And Forms!Kartica!txtDatumDo)) Or (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "435*") And ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between Forms!Kartica!txtDatumOd And Forms!Kartica!txtDatumDo))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '', LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.Name, Company.PrintName, Company.InvoicePostalCode, Company.InvoiceCity, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber, InvoiceBatch.Label, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId
HAVING (((Company.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Kartica]![cmbSZ] Or (Company.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Kartica]![cmbSZ]))
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
izv_kratica_zbirna_kupca_bck20251004
-----SQL-----
SELECT Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '' AS Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef AS DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, First(LedgerEntry.InvoiceId) AS FirstOfRACID, First(Invoice.IssueDate) AS FirstOfDatumIzdavanja, Company.Name, Partner.Name
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "435*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '', LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.Name, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId
ORDER BY Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
izv_kratica_zbirna_kupca_det
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Date, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '' AS Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef AS DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId & " - " & LedgerEntry.Date AS Grupa1, Company.CompanyId
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN LineItemType ON LedgerEntry.LineTypeId = LineItemType.LineItemTypeId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between [Forms]![Kartica]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Kartica]![txtDatumDo])) OR (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "435*") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between [Forms]![Kartica]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Kartica]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '', LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId & " - " & LedgerEntry.Date, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId
HAVING (((Company.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Kartica]![cmbSZ] Or (Company.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Kartica]![cmbSZ]))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
izv_kratica_zbirna_kupca_det_bck20251004
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Date, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '' AS Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef AS DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN LineItemType ON LedgerEntry.LineTypeId = LineItemType.LineItemTypeId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '', LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
izv_kratica_zbirna_kuppca_det_dob
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Date, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '' AS Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef AS DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType
FROM (((LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Invoice ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN LineItemType ON LedgerEntry.LineTypeId = LineItemType.LineItemTypeId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, '', LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Invoice.IssueDate, Company.Name, Partner.Name, LineItemType.LineItemType, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date, Company.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)) AS Balance, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.Balance) AS SumOfStanje
SELECT Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.CompanyId, Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.Balance) AS [Total Of Balance]
FROM Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca
GROUP BY Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.CompanyId, Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.PartnerAccountingId
PIVOT Izvestaj_GK_Placanje_Dobavljaca.PostingCode;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_GK_Racuni
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, Sum(Round(DebitAmount-CreditAmount,2)) AS Balance, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.Partner
FROM (Invoice INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON Invoice.InvoiceId = LedgerEntry.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.Partner
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.InvoiceId)>0));


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_GK_Racuni_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.Balance) AS SumOfStanje
SELECT Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.CompanyId, Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.PartnerId, Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.Partner, Sum(Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.Balance) AS Total
FROM Izvestaj_GK_Racuni
GROUP BY Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.CompanyId, Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.PartnerId, Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.Partner
PIVOT Izvestaj_GK_Racuni.Code;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_Izvod
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix AS FiscalYear, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS KONTOPARTNERA, Partner.Name AS NazivPartnera, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name AS NazivTroska, BankStatementLine.Id, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId
FROM ((((BankStatement INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
GROUP BY BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, BankStatementLine.Id, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, BankStatementLine.LineNumber
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date, BankStatementLine.LineNumber;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_Izvod_GRP
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix AS FiscalYear, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId AS KONTOPARTNERA, Partner.Name AS NazivPartnera, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, BankStatementLine.Id
FROM (((BankStatement INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON BankStatement.BankStatementId = BankStatementLine.BankStatementId) INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatement.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON BankStatementLine.Id = LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY BankStatement.CompanyId, Company.Name, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, BankStatementLine.Id, BankStatementLine.LineNumber
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date, BankStatementLine.LineNumber;


=====QUERY=====
Izvestaj_Racuni
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(Invoice.Total) AS SumOfUkupno
SELECT Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.Partner, Sum(Invoice.Total) AS [Total Of Total]
FROM Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, Invoice.Partner
PIVOT InvoiceBatch.Code;


=====QUERY=====
IZVESTAJ_STANJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.Account, Last(LedgerEntry.Date) AS Poslednja_uplata, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber, Company.RegistrationNumber, Company.Address, Company.PostalCode, Company.PrintName
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Partner.DOB)=0))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber, Company.RegistrationNumber, Company.Address, Company.PostalCode, Company.PrintName
ORDER BY Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
IZVOD_UPLATE_RACUN
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=1))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "204*") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between #6/1/2026# And #6/30/2026#))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.Date;


=====QUERY=====
IzvodiStavke
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.Date, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.StatementSuffix, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.JournalEntryLineNumber, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.BankAccountNumber, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.Code, BankStatementLine.PaymentReference, BankStatementLine.PartnerAccountingId, GK_Po_Izvodu.JournalEntryNumber, GK_Po_Izvodu.PartnerId, GK_Po_Izvodu.Name, GK_Po_Izvodu.SumOfPIZNOS, GK_Po_Izvodu.SumOfDIZNOS, GK_Po_Izvodu.BrojUlazaUGK
FROM GK_Po_Izvodu RIGHT JOIN (BankStatementLine INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) ON GK_Po_Izvodu.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date;


=====QUERY=====
IzvodiStavke_Simple
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.Date, GK_Po_Izvodu.PartnerId, GK_Po_Izvodu.Name, GK_Po_Izvodu.SumOfPIZNOS, GK_Po_Izvodu.SumOfDIZNOS
FROM GK_Po_Izvodu INNER JOIN (BankStatementLine INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId) ON GK_Po_Izvodu.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id
ORDER BY BankStatement.Date;


=====QUERY=====
Izvodi-Sume
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit) AS SumOfZaduzenje, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje, BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.Date
FROM BankStatementLine INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId=BankStatement.BankStatementId
GROUP BY BankStatement.Debit, BankStatement.Credit, BankStatement.CreditJournalEntryId, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatement.CompanyId, BankStatement.Date;


=====QUERY=====
IZVOD-NERASPOREDJENA-SREDSTVA
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.Id, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, (IIf(First(LedgerEntry.Account) Like "121112?",((Credit-Debit)*2)+Max(CreditAmount)+Max(DebitAmount),(Credit-Debit)-(Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount))))=0 AS Kontrola, BankStatementLine.BankStatementId
FROM (LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN BankStatementLine ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) INNER JOIN BankStatement ON BankStatementLine.BankStatementId = BankStatement.BankStatementId
GROUP BY BankStatementLine.Id, BankStatement.StatementNumber, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.LineNumber, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId, BankStatementLine.Debit, BankStatementLine.Credit, BankStatementLine.BankStatementId
HAVING ((((IIf(First(LedgerEntry.Account) Like "121112?",((Credit-Debit)*2)+Max(CreditAmount)+Max(DebitAmount),(Credit-Debit)-(Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount))))=0) Is Null))
ORDER BY BankStatementLine.LineNumber ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
IzvodPrilivi
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Building, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje
FROM BankStatementLine INNER JOIN Company ON BankStatementLine.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY Company.CompanyId, Company.Building, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate;


=====QUERY=====
IZVODSTAVKE_SUME
-----SQL-----
SELECT BankStatementLine.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit) AS SumOfZaduzenje, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje
FROM BankStatementLine
GROUP BY BankStatementLine.BankStatementId, BankStatementLine.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
IzvodSumaZO

SELECT BankStatementLine.JournalEntryLineNumber, Sum(BankStatementLine.Debit) AS SumOfZaduzenje, Sum(BankStatementLine.Credit) AS SumOfOdobrenje, BankStatementLine.CompanyId
FROM BankStatementLine
GROUP BY BankStatementLine.JournalEntryLineNumber, BankStatementLine.CompanyId;
