=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_PLACANJA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Balance
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "4350*") And ((LedgerEntry.Date)>=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumOd And (LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumDo))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "4350*") And ((LedgerEntry.Date)>=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumOd And (LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Izvestaji!txtDatumDo))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB_L2
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Partner.Name
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "4350*") AND ((LedgerEntry.Date)>=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And (LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Partner.Name
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "2040*"))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR_L2
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.Account
FROM LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, LedgerEntry.Account
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_SUME
-----SQL-----
SELECT SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfPIZNOS AS POTRAZUJU_DOB, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfDIZNOS AS PLACENO_DOB, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfDIZNOS AS DUGUJU_KUPCI, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfPIZNOS AS NAPLACENO_KUPCIMA
FROM (SubAccount LEFT JOIN KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR ON SubAccount.SubAccountCode = KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SubAccount) LEFT JOIN KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB ON SubAccount.SubAccountCode = KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SubAccount
WHERE ((Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfPIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfDIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfDIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfPIZNOS) Is Null) AND ((Len(SubAccountCode))=4));


=====QUERY=====
KONTO_TROSKA_SUME_L2
-----SQL-----
SELECT SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfPIZNOS AS POTRAZUJU_DOB, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfDIZNOS AS PLACENO_DOB, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfDIZNOS AS DUGUJU_KUPCI, KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfPIZNOS AS NAPLACENO_KUPCIMA
FROM (SubAccount LEFT JOIN KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR ON SubAccount.SubAccountCode = KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SubAccount) LEFT JOIN KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB ON SubAccount.SubAccountCode = KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SubAccount
WHERE ((Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfPIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_DOB.SumOfDIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfDIZNOS) Is Null) AND (Not (KONTO_TROSKA_POTRAZIVANJA_STANAR.SumOfPIZNOS) Is Null) AND ((Len(SubAccountCode))=4));


=====QUERY=====
KONTOLA-STAVKE-MESEC-RDOB-RDOBPRIKAZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT [KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId, Count([KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].Code) AS CountOfGrupaRacunaFXN, [KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].CompanyId
FROM [KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId]
GROUP BY [KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].CompanyId
HAVING (((Count([KONTROLA-STAVKE-Month-SupplierInvoiceId].Code))<>1));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA_001_GK_241
-----SQL-----
SELECT frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS BaseAmount
FROM frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS
GROUP BY frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account
HAVING (((frmIzvestajFilterGK_TS.Account) Like "241*"));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA_001_IZVOD_241
-----SQL-----
SELECT I.CompanyId, I.Date, I.Note, I.NewBalance, X.SumDuguje, X.SumPotrazuje
FROM BankStatement AS I INNER JOIN (SELECT 
        Izvod2.Note,
        Max(Izvod2.Date) AS MaxOfDatum, Sum(Debit) as SumDuguje, Sum(Credit) as SumPotrazuje
    FROM BankStatement AS Izvod2
    WHERE Izvod2.CompanyId=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]
    AND Izvod2.Date <= [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]
    GROUP BY Izvod2.Note
)  AS X ON (I.Note = X.Note OR (I.Note IS NULL AND X.Note IS NULL)) AND (I.Date = X.MaxOfDatum)
WHERE (((I.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Izvestaji]![cmbSZ]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA_DUPLI_RACUNI_DOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
FROM LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "4*"))
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
HAVING (((Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId))>1));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA_SUME_TK
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS BaseAmount, Left(SubAccount,2) AS KT, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Left(SubAccount,2);


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-FILTER-GK-PARAMETAR
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.*
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Parameters)=[unesi-param]))
ORDER BY IsNull(SubAccount), LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount;


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-GK-GRP-RDOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId)=0) AND ((LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId)=0))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId)>0));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-IMAM-GK-NEMARSTAVKE
-----SQL-----
SELECT [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].CompanyId, [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SumOfDIZNOS, [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SumOfPIZNOS, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId
FROM ([KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId] LEFT JOIN [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId] ON [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId = [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE ((([KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-IMAM-RSTAVKE-NEMAGK
-----SQL-----
SELECT [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].Code, [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].CompanyId, [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId
FROM [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId] RIGHT JOIN [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId] ON [KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId = [KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE ((([KONTROLA-LedgerEntry-GRP-SupplierInvoiceId].SupplierInvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId AS KontrolaSK, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
FROM ((((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.Code)=[UNESI-InvoiceMonth]) AND ((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=[UNESI-CompanyId]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-GK-RDOB-ISPRAVKA
-----SQL-----
SELECT "R-" & InvoiceMonth AS Expr1, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)=[SKID]));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceMonth=Code AS Expr1, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM (InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceMonth=Code, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE-gk
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].Code, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].InvoiceMonth, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].CompanyId, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].Expr1, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].SupplierInvoiceId
FROM [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE] INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].SupplierInvoiceId = LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].Code, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].InvoiceMonth, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].CompanyId, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].SupplierInvoiceId, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].Expr1, [KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-RACUNISTAVKE].SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-KNJIZENJA-RACUNA-SVE
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId AS KontrolaSK, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS PostingCount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM ((((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2040" Or (LedgerEntry.Account)="4350"))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SubAccount.Name, Company.Name, CompanyId=CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Partner.Name, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.PostedInvoiceAmount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
ORDER BY InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth;


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-RDOB-NEMA-STAVKI
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.Supplier, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CalculationType, SupplierInvoice.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId
FROM SupplierInvoice LEFT JOIN InvoiceLine ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceLineId) Is Null))
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, SupplierInvoice.Supplier, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.CalculationType, SupplierInvoice.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker, SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId
HAVING (((SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth) Like "19*"));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-GRP-RDOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Count(InvoiceLine.InvoiceLineId) AS CountOfIDRacunStavke, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, Sum(InvoiceLine.InvoiceAmount) AS SumOfIZNOSRACUNA
FROM InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.PostingJournalEntryId)<>0));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-RSTAVKE-PB
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Code, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Count(InvoiceLine.InvoiceLineId) AS CountOfIDRacunStavke, Replace(PaymentReference,"-","") AS Expr1
FROM (InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Invoice ON (InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) AND (InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Replace(PaymentReference,"-","")
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=106));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-STAVKE-MESEC-RDOB
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Count(InvoiceLine.InvoiceLineId) AS CountOfIDRacunStavke
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-SUMA-KONTO-2410
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.Account)="2410") AND ((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=101));


=====QUERY=====
KONTROLA-UPLATE-NA-STORNO-RACUN
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceId, GK_2040_RACID_SUM.CompanyId, Invoice.IsCancelled, GK_2040_RACID_SUM.SumOfDIZNOS, GK_2040_RACID_SUM.SumOfPIZNOS, Invoice.Total, Round([SumOfDIZNOS]-Total,2) AS RAZLIKA
FROM GK_2040_RACID_SUM INNER JOIN Invoice ON GK_2040_RACID_SUM.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId;


=====QUERY=====
KorisnikSZ
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Partner.CompanyId
FROM Partner
WHERE (((Partner.PartnerId)<8000) AND ((Partner.CompanyId)=1));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac Without Matching Objekti
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.*
FROM Partner LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
WHERE (((Partner.PartnerId)<8000) AND ((Unit.PartnerId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_Dug
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId AS PartnerAccountingId, Nz([Kupac_Dug_0].Sum,0) AS Suma0, Nz([Kupac_Dug_98].Sum,0) AS Suma98
FROM (Partner LEFT JOIN Kupac_DUG_0 ON Partner.PartnerId = Kupac_DUG_0.PartnerAccountingId) LEFT JOIN Kupac_DUG_98 ON Partner.PartnerId = Kupac_DUG_98.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_DUG_0
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId, GK_Filter_PD.CompanyId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Sum, GK_Filter_PD.Account
FROM GK_Filter_PD
WHERE (((GK_Filter_PD.GTS)=0 Or (GK_Filter_PD.GTS)=99))
GROUP BY GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId, GK_Filter_PD.CompanyId, GK_Filter_PD.Account;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_DUG_97
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Sum
FROM GK_Filter_PD
WHERE (((GK_Filter_PD.GTS)=97))
GROUP BY GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_DUG_98
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Sum
FROM GK_Filter_PD
WHERE (((GK_Filter_PD.GTS)=98))
GROUP BY GK_Filter_PD.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_Dug_OLD
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Nz([Kupac_Dug_0].Sum,0) AS Suma0, Nz([Kupac_Dug_98].Sum,0) AS Suma98
FROM (LedgerEntry LEFT JOIN Kupac_DUG_0 ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Kupac_DUG_0.PartnerAccountingId) LEFT JOIN Kupac_DUG_98 ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Kupac_DUG_98.PartnerAccountingId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Nz([Kupac_Dug_0].Sum,0), Nz([Kupac_Dug_98].Sum,0);


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_DUG_tempbackup
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Sum
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)>98 And (LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)<95)) OR (((LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)>98 And (LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)<95))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_GK_GRP
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_GM
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitId, Unit.CompanyId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.HandOverDate, Unit.Status, Unit.Name
FROM Unit
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_GRUPNI_RACUN_MASTERID
-----SQL-----
SELECT Kupac_1.PartnerId AS LegacyMasterPartnerId, Count(Partner.PartnerId) AS BROj_PODRACUNA, Kupac_1.CompanyId
FROM Partner INNER JOIN Partner AS Kupac_1 ON Partner.GroupInvoiceTagId = Kupac_1.PartnerId
GROUP BY Kupac_1.PartnerId, Kupac_1.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_NEMA_EMAIL_SLANJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT MailAktivni.PartnerId, MailNeAktivni.FirstOfeMail, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode
FROM ((Partner LEFT JOIN MailAktivni ON Partner.PartnerId = MailAktivni.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN MailNeAktivni ON Partner.PartnerId = MailNeAktivni.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId
WHERE (((MailAktivni.PartnerId) Is Null) AND ((Partner.PartnerId)<8000));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_Opomena_001
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_Filter_PD.*
FROM GK_Filter_PD
WHERE (((GK_Filter_PD.Date)<=DebitPeriodDate) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.DebitAmount)>0) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.Flt)=0)) OR (((GK_Filter_PD.Date)<=CreditPeriodDate) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.DebitAmount)<0) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.Flt)=0)) OR (((GK_Filter_PD.Date)<=CreditPeriodDate) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.CreditAmount)<>0) AND ((GK_Filter_PD.Flt)=0));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_PBPD
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Replace([PBPD],"-","") AS ReferenceCode
FROM Partner
WHERE (((Partner.PBPD) Is Not Null));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_PD
-----SQL-----
SELECT Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS PD, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID
FROM Kupac_PBPD INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId=LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
GROUP BY Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
Kupac_PD_GDATUM
-----SQL-----
SELECT Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS PD, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID
FROM Kupac_PBPD INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId = LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
GROUP BY Kupac_PBPD.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_STAN
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitId, Unit.CompanyId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.HandOverDate, Unit.Status, Unit.Name
FROM Unit
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=1));


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_TS
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId AS ExchangeRateId, Round(Nz([PD],0),2) AS PreviousBalance, Round(Nz(Sum,0)-Nz([PD],0),2) AS Balance, Round(Sum,2) AS SumaR
FROM Kupac_DUG RIGHT JOIN (Kupac_PD RIGHT JOIN Partner ON Kupac_PD.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) ON Kupac_DUG.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
KUPAC_TS_GDATUM
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId AS ExchangeRateId, Round(Nz([PD],0),2) AS BLOK, Round(Nz(Sum,0)-Nz([PD],0),2) AS Balance, Round(Sum,2) AS SumaR
FROM Kupac_DUG RIGHT JOIN (Kupac_PD RIGHT JOIN Partner ON Kupac_PD.PartnerId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Kupac_DUG.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
KupacBezSlanja
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.SkipPrintInvoiceGroup, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.Name
FROM Partner
WHERE (((Partner.SkipPrintInvoiceGroup)=-1));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac-Email-CR
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, IIf(Not IsNull(PartnerId),ConcatRelated("eMail","Mail","IDPartner = " & PartnerId)) AS Emails
FROM Partner INNER JOIN PartnerEmail ON Partner.PartnerId = PartnerEmail.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
KupacLanjeEmailGroup
-----SQL-----
SELECT KupacSlanjeMail.PartnerId
FROM KupacSlanjeMail
GROUP BY KupacSlanjeMail.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
kupac-objekti-cr
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.Name, ConcatRelated("naziv","Objekti","lnk_ID_K = " & Nz(PartnerId,0)) AS Unit
FROM Partner INNER JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
GROUP BY Partner.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.Name;


=====QUERY=====
KupacSlanjeMail
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId, PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail, PartnerEmail.Email
FROM PartnerEmail
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail)=-1));


=====QUERY=====
Kupac-Za-Utuzenje
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, AdresaDostave.Address AS AdresaStana, AdresaDostave.Building, Partner.Address, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.PartnerCity, Partner.Note, [Partner-Email-CR].Emails, [Partner-Unit-cr].Unit, AdresaDostave.UnitTypeShortCode
FROM (((SelectionBasket INNER JOIN Partner ON SelectionBasket.TargetId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN [Partner-Email-CR] ON Partner.PartnerId = [Partner-Email-CR].PartnerId) LEFT JOIN [Partner-Unit-cr] ON Partner.PartnerId = [Partner-Unit-cr].PartnerId) LEFT JOIN AdresaDostave ON Partner.PartnerId = AdresaDostave.PartnerId
WHERE (((SelectionBasket.TypeIndex)=11));


=====QUERY=====
KupciSkupstine
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId
FROM Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
Last_GrupaOpomena
-----SQL-----
SELECT Last(ReminderBatch.ReminderBatchId) AS LastOfIDGrupaOpomena
FROM ReminderBatch;


=====QUERY=====
LastRacuniTroskovi
-----SQL-----
SELECT ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceMonth
FROM ExpenseInvoice
GROUP BY ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceMonth, Right(InvoiceMonth,2), Left(InvoiceMonth,2)
ORDER BY Right(InvoiceMonth,2) ShortDescription , Left(InvoiceMonth,2) ShortDescription;


=====QUERY=====
LIST_001_SKUPSTINA-OBJEKTI
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Name AS Skupstina, Company.SortOrder AS SortRB, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS UnitCount
FROM Company INNER JOIN Unit ON Company.CompanyId = Unit.CompanyId
GROUP BY Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
LIST_002_SKUPSTINA-KUPCI
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Name AS Skupstina, Company.SortOrder AS SortRB, Count(Partner.PartnerId) AS BrojKupaca
FROM Partner INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Company.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
LISTING_KUPAC_OBJEKTI
-----SQL-----
SELECT KUPAC_STAN.Name, Partner.Name, KUPAC_STAN.HandOverDate, KUPAC_STAN.Status, KUPAC_GM.Name, KUPAC_GM.HandOverDate, KUPAC_GM.Status, Partner.CompanyId
FROM (KUPAC_STAN INNER JOIN Partner ON KUPAC_STAN.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN KUPAC_GM ON Partner.PartnerId = KUPAC_GM.PartnerId
ORDER BY KUPAC_STAN.HandOverDate;


=====QUERY=====
LISTING_KUPAC_OBJEKTI_CNT
-----SQL-----
SELECT KUPAC_STAN.Name, Partner.Name, KUPAC_STAN.HandOverDate, KUPAC_STAN.Status, Partner.CompanyId, Count(KUPAC_GM.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O
FROM (KUPAC_STAN INNER JOIN Partner ON KUPAC_STAN.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN KUPAC_GM ON Partner.PartnerId = KUPAC_GM.PartnerId
GROUP BY KUPAC_STAN.Name, Partner.Name, KUPAC_STAN.HandOverDate, KUPAC_STAN.Status, Partner.CompanyId
ORDER BY KUPAC_STAN.HandOverDate;


=====QUERY=====
Mail_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM First(PartnerEmail.Email) AS FirstOfeMail
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId, First(PartnerEmail.Email) AS [Total Of Email]
FROM PartnerEmail
GROUP BY PartnerEmail.PartnerId
PIVOT PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail;


=====QUERY=====
MailAktivni
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId, First(PartnerEmail.Email) AS FirstOfeMail, Count(PartnerEmail.PartnerEmailId) AS CountOfIDeMail
FROM PartnerEmail
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail)=-1))
GROUP BY PartnerEmail.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
MailGrupByIDK
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, SpojRedove("email","mail","IDPartner=" & Nz(PartnerId,0),"SortOrder","; ") AS MailGrup, SpojRedove("email","mail","SendMailRacun=-1 AND IDPartner=" & Nz(PartnerId,0),"SortOrder","; ") AS MailGrupRacun
FROM Partner;


=====QUERY=====
MailNeAktivni
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.PartnerId, First(PartnerEmail.Email) AS FirstOfeMail, Count(PartnerEmail.PartnerEmailId) AS CountOfIDeMail
FROM PartnerEmail
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail)=0))
GROUP BY PartnerEmail.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
MultiMailInOne
-----SQL-----
SELECT PartnerEmail.*
FROM PartnerEmail
WHERE (((PartnerEmail.Email) Like '*;*'));


=====QUERY=====
Nalog_Tip_98
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=98));


=====QUERY=====
Objekat_Sume
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeId, Sum([K1]*[K2]) AS [SUM-K1K2], Sum(Unit.K2) AS [SUM-K2], Sum(Unit.K3) AS [SUM-K3], Sum(Unit.K4) AS [SUM-K4], Sum(Unit.K5) AS [SUM-K5]
FROM Unit
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeId;


=====QUERY=====
Objekat_Sume_Po_TipovimaIzRacuna
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekat_Sume.CompanyId, Sum(Objekat_Sume.[SUM-K1K2]) AS [SUM-K1K2], Sum(Objekat_Sume.[SUM-K2]) AS [SUM-K2], Sum(Objekat_Sume.[SUM-K3]) AS [SUM-K3], Sum(Objekat_Sume.[SUM-K4]) AS [SUM-K4], Sum(Objekat_Sume.[SUM-K5]) AS [SUM-K5]
FROM SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN (Objekat_Sume INNER JOIN SupplierInvoiceUnitType ON Objekat_Sume.UnitTypeId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.UnitType) ON (SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId) AND (SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = Objekat_Sume.CompanyId)
GROUP BY SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekat_Sume.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
Objekat_Sume_Sve
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId AS CompanyId, Sum(Unit.K1) AS [SUM-K1], Sum(Unit.K2) AS [SUM-K2], Sum(Unit.K3) AS [SUM-K3], Sum(Unit.K4) AS [SUM-K4], Sum(Unit.K5) AS [SUM-K5]
FROM Unit
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
Objekti_By_KupacID
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O
FROM Unit
GROUP BY Unit.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
Objekti_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS [Total Of UnitId]
FROM Unit
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId
PIVOT Unit.UnitTypeId;


=====QUERY=====
Objekti_Prebrojavanje
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, Sum(Unit.K1) AS SumOfK1, Sum(Unit.K2) AS SumOfK2, Sum(Unit.K3) AS SumOfK3, Sum(Unit.K4) AS SumOfK4, Sum(Unit.K5) AS SumOfK5
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId
PIVOT UnitType.Name;


=====QUERY=====
Objekti_Sort
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.Name, Unit.UnitId, Unit.PartnerId, SortOrder & Name AS SortN
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY Unit.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.Name;


=====QUERY=====
OBJEKTI_SS_TIP
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.SortOrder, Company.Name, Unit.UnitId, Unit.Quantity, UnitType.PrintLabel, Unit.PartnerId, Quantity*[JO] AS Price
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY Company.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
OBJEKTI_SS_TIP_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.Quantity) AS SumOfkolicina
SELECT OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.SortOrder, OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.Name, Sum(OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.Price) AS SumOfCENA
FROM OBJEKTI_SS_TIP
GROUP BY OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.SortOrder, OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.Name
ORDER BY OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.SortOrder
PIVOT OBJEKTI_SS_TIP.PrintLabel;


=====QUERY=====
OBJEKTI_STAT
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.SortOrder, Company.Name, Unit.UnitId, Unit.Quantity AS Expr1, UnitType.PrintLabel, Unit.PartnerId
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY Company.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
Objekti_Za_Racun
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.UnitTypeId, UnitType.SortOrder, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS UnitCount, Sum(Unit.K1) AS SumOfK1, Sum(Unit.K2) AS SumOfK2, Sum(Unit.K3) AS SumOfK3, Sum(Unit.K4) AS SumOfK4, Sum(Unit.K5) AS SumOfK5, Sum([K2]*[K1]) AS K21, Sum([K2]*[K3]) AS K23, Sum([K2]*[K4]) AS K24, Sum([K2]*[K5]) AS K25
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=1))
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.UnitTypeId, UnitType.SortOrder
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
OBJEKTI-GM-KUPAC
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitTypeId, Partner.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, Unit.UnitId
FROM Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));


=====QUERY=====
oBJEKTIgpm
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.UnitId, Unit.Name, Unit.UnitTypeId, UnitType.Name, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Partner.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.Status, Unit.K1, Unit.K2
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));


=====QUERY=====
ObjektiKupca
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, UnitType.SortOrder, UnitType.PrintLabel
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
ObjektiSaTipom
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.Name, UnitType.Name, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Unit.UnitTypeCode, SortOrder*1000000+Nz(UnitTypeCode,0) & Name AS SorterFn
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY Unit.UnitTypeCode, SortOrder*1000000+Nz(UnitTypeCode,0) & Name;


=====QUERY=====
ObjektiSaTipomKoef
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitId, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.Name, UnitType.Name, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.K3, Unit.K4, Unit.K5, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Unit.ResidentCount, Unit.Entrance, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.Status
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode;


=====QUERY=====
ObjektiSaTipomPrvi
-----SQL-----
SELECT ObjektiSaTipom.PartnerId, First(ObjektiSaTipom.Name) AS FirstOfnaziv, Min(ObjektiSaTipom.UnitTypeCode) AS MinOfBrojPD
FROM ObjektiSaTipom
GROUP BY ObjektiSaTipom.PartnerId
ORDER BY Min(ObjektiSaTipom.UnitTypeCode);


=====QUERY=====
OBJEKTI-STAN-KUPAC
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.UnitTypeId, Partner.Name, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.CompanyId, Partner.PartnerId, Unit.UnitId
FROM Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=1));


=====QUERY=====
ObjektiSviPoKupcu
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.Name & ' - ' & Name AS Expr1, Partner.Name, Company.Name, ConcatRelated('naziv','Objekti','lnk_ID_K=' & PartnerId) AS Unit
FROM (Partner LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Partner.CompanyId)=152))
GROUP BY Partner.PartnerId, Partner.CompanyId, Partner.Name & ' - ' & Name, Partner.Name, Company.Name;


=====QUERY=====
OpomenaExportListing
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Building, Company.Name, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Partner.Address, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.TaxId, Partner.RegistrationNumber, Reminder.PaymentReference, Reminder.UnpaidInvoiceCount, Reminder.Debt, Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Reminder.LineItemSum, Partner.PartnerCity, Company.Address, BuildingEntrance.Address, BuildingEntrance.Entrance
FROM ((((SelectionBasket INNER JOIN Reminder ON SelectionBasket.TargetId = Reminder.ReminderId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Reminder.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN BuildingEntrance ON Unit.Entrance = BuildingEntrance.Entrance
WHERE (((SelectionBasket.TypeIndex)=2));

OPOMENA-FILL-TROSKOVI

UPDATE Reminder SET Reminder.Costs = 4500, Reminder.Total = Debt+4500
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=[GRUPA Reminder Id]));

OpomenaZaRacun

SELECT ReminderBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Reminder.PartnerAccountingId, Reminder.InvoiceText, Reminder.Debt, Reminder.IsActive
FROM ReminderBatch INNER JOIN Reminder ON ReminderBatch.ReminderBatchId = Reminder.ReminderBatchId
WHERE (((Reminder.IsActive)=-1));


=====QUERY=====
OPOMENE_PARTNERI_SALDA
-----SQL-----
SELECT GK_PARTNERI.PartnerAccountingId, GK_PARTNERI.SumOfDIZNOS, GK_PARTNERI.SumOfPIZNOS, GK_PARTNERI.BaseAmount
FROM Reminder INNER JOIN GK_PARTNERI ON Reminder.PartnerAccountingId = GK_PARTNERI.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=20)) OR (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=21)) OR (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=22));


=====QUERY=====
OpomeneListingContact
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Building, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Partner.Address, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.RegistrationNumber, Partner.TaxId, Partner.Note, SkipPrintInvoiceGroup=0 AS ŠtampaniRacun, Unit.Name AS DostavaŠtampani, BuildingEntrance.Address AS DostavaŠtampaniAdresa, PartnerEmail.Email, PartnerEmail.SendInvoiceByEmail AS RacunNaEmail, Partner.PartnerCity
FROM (SelectionBasket INNER JOIN Reminder ON SelectionBasket.TargetId = Reminder.ReminderId) INNER JOIN (((Partner LEFT JOIN PartnerEmail ON Partner.PartnerId = PartnerEmail.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN (Unit LEFT JOIN BuildingEntrance ON Unit.Entrance = BuildingEntrance.Entrance) ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) ON Reminder.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((SelectionBasket.TypeIndex)=2))
ORDER BY Partner.PartnerId, PartnerEmail.SortOrder;

PARKING
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Building, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.NetArea
FROM Company INNER JOIN Unit ON Company.CompanyId = Unit.CompanyId
WHERE (((Unit.NetArea)>12.5) AND ((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4))
ORDER BY Company.Building, Unit.UnitTypeCode;

Prebrojavanje
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeId, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O, Sum(Unit.K1) AS SumOfK1, Sum(Unit.K2) AS SumOfK2, Sum(Unit.Coefficient) AS SumOfKoeficijent
FROM Unit INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId, UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeId;

PREBROJAVANJE-GK-PO-SK

SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS CountOfSTAVKAID
FROM Company LEFT JOIN LedgerEntry ON Company.CompanyId = LedgerEntry.CompanyId

prenos-povezivanje

FROM Partner RIGHT JOIN BalanceCarryForward ON Partner.ExternalAccount = BalanceCarryForward.ReferenceCode;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Filter_Opomena
-----SQL-----
SELECT Reminder.*
FROM Reminder
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Combo43));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Filter_Opomena_EXPORT
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.CompanyId, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.Name, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.PartnerId, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.Name, Nz([R1],0)-Nz([R2],0) AS [Prethodno Balance], Nz([R2],0) AS [Poslednji izdat Invoice], Nz([R3],0) AS [Uplaceno u tekucem mesecu], Nz([R4],0) AS [Preostali Debt], Nz([R5],0) AS [ParentAccount Debt za BLOK 67], Nz([R6],0) AS [Uplaceno za BLOK 67], Nz([R7],0) AS [Preostali Debt za BLOK 67], Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.CKOP AS [Reminder pred tužbu]
FROM Presek_Filter_Opomena_R;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Filter_Opomena_EXPORT_BySKID
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.CompanyId, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.Name, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.PartnerId, Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.Name, Nz([R1],0)-Nz([R2],0) AS [Prethodno Balance], Nz([R2],0) AS [Poslednji izdat Invoice], Nz([R3],0) AS [Uplaceno u tekucem mesecu], Nz([R4],0) AS [Preostali Debt], Nz([R5],0) AS [ParentAccount Debt za BLOK 67], Nz([R6],0) AS [Uplaceno za BLOK 67], Nz([R7],0) AS [Preostali Debt za BLOK 67], Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.CKOP AS [Reminder pred tužbu]
FROM Presek_Filter_Opomena_R
WHERE (((Presek_Filter_Opomena_R.CompanyId)=[Forms]![Presek_Izvestaj]![Combo37]));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Filter_Opomena_R
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Presek_Sub_001.Sum AS R1, Presek_Sub_002.DebitAmount AS R2, Presek_Sub_003.Sum AS R3, Presek_Sub_004.Sum AS R4, Presek_Sub_005.Sum AS R5, Presek_Sub_006.Sum AS R6, Presek_Sub_007.Sum AS R7, Company.PrintName, Not IsNull(ReminderId) AS CKOP
FROM Presek_Filter_Opomena RIGHT JOIN ((Presek_Sub_007 RIGHT JOIN (Presek_Sub_006 RIGHT JOIN (Presek_Sub_005 RIGHT JOIN (Presek_Sub_004 RIGHT JOIN (Presek_Sub_003 RIGHT JOIN (Presek_Sub_002 RIGHT JOIN ((Presek_Sub_001 RIGHT JOIN Partner ON Presek_Sub_001.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId) ON Presek_Sub_002.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Presek_Sub_003.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Presek_Sub_004.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Presek_Sub_005.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Presek_Sub_006.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) ON Presek_Sub_007.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Objekti_By_KupacID ON Partner.PartnerId=Objekti_By_KupacID.PartnerId) ON Presek_Filter_Opomena.PartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Partner.Name) Not Like "nepoznat*"))
ORDER BY Partner.PartnerId;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_001
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.CompanyId AS CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Parameters) Is Null) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status!Date) And ((Partner.PartnerId)<8000)) Or (((LedgerEntry.Parameters) Not Like Nz(Partner.PBPD_F,"")) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status!Date) And ((Partner.PartnerId)<8000))
GROUP BY Partner.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount))>=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]![MinIznos] Or (Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount))>=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]![MinIznos]));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_002
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS Sum, IIf(IsNull(Parameters),'',Mid(Parameters,3,10)) AS rPAR, Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.Sum AS U, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Presek_Opomene_Sub_001 ON Partner.PartnerId = Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)<=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!Date))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, IIf(IsNull(Parameters),'',Mid(Parameters,3,10)), Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.Sum;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_003
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.PartnerAccountingId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.Sum, Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.rPAR, Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.U
FROM Presek_Opomene_Sub_002
WHERE (((Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.Sum)<-[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!DebtTolerance));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_004
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.PartnerAccountingId, Count(Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.Sum) AS CountOfSuma
FROM Presek_Opomene_Sub_002
WHERE (((Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.Sum)>[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!DebtTolerance))
GROUP BY Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((Count(Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.Sum))>=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!MinUnpaidInvoiceCount));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_005
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.CompanyId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_004.PartnerAccountingId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_004.CountOfSuma AS UnpaidInvoiceCount, Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.Sum
FROM Presek_Opomene_Sub_004 INNER JOIN Presek_Opomene_Sub_001 ON Presek_Opomene_Sub_004.PartnerAccountingId = Presek_Opomene_Sub_001.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_006
-----SQL-----
SELECT Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.*, Presek_Opomene_Sub_005.UnpaidInvoiceCount, IIf(IsNull([rPAR]),0,Mid([rPAR],5,2)) AS M, IIf(IsNull([rPAR]),Year(Forms!Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status!Date),Mid([rPAR],1,4)) AS Y, IIf(IsNull([rPAR]) Or [rPar]='',300,CInt(Mid([rPAR],5,2))+100) AS Text
FROM Presek_Opomene_Sub_002 INNER JOIN Presek_Opomene_Sub_005 ON Presek_Opomene_Sub_002.PartnerAccountingId = Presek_Opomene_Sub_005.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_007
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Reminder ( PartnerAccountingId, UnpaidInvoiceCount, Debt, ReminderBatchId, PaymentReference )
SELECT Presek_Opomene_Sub_005.PartnerAccountingId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_005.UnpaidInvoiceCount, Presek_Opomene_Sub_005.Sum, Forms!Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status!ReminderBatchId AS Expr1, KontrolniBroj(97,CompanyId & "-" & PartnerAccountingId & "-P" & Format([Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!Date,"yyyymmdd")) & "-" & CompanyId & "-" & PartnerAccountingId & "-P" & Format([Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!Date,"yyyymmdd") AS KB
FROM Presek_Opomene_Sub_005;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_008
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO ReminderLine ( ReminderId, PartnerAccountingId, M, Y, Debit, Credit, Sum, Text )
SELECT Reminder.ReminderId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.PartnerAccountingId, Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.M, Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.Y, Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.SumOfDIZNOS, Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.SumOfPIZNOS, Round([SumOfDIZNOS]-[SumOfPIZNOS],2) AS Sm, Message & ' ' & [Y] & '.' AS Expr2
FROM Reminder INNER JOIN (Presek_Opomene_Sub_006 INNER JOIN Dictionary ON Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.Text = Dictionary.SortIndex) ON Reminder.PartnerAccountingId = Presek_Opomene_Sub_006.PartnerAccountingId
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!ReminderBatchId) AND ((Dictionary.Language)="SR-LAT-NC"));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_009
-----SQL-----
DELETE Reminder.ReminderBatchId, ReminderLine.*
FROM ReminderLine INNER JOIN Reminder ON ReminderLine.ReminderId = Reminder.ReminderId
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!ReminderBatchId));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Opomene_Sub_010
-----SQL-----
DELETE Reminder.ReminderBatchId, Reminder.*
FROM Reminder
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=[Forms]![Opomene_WorkInProgres_Status]!ReminderBatchId));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Sub_000
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)=98) And ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=98) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text13))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Sub_001
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount) AS Sum
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)<>98) And ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)<>98) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text13))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Sub_002
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Date)=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text41) And ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=3))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId
HAVING (((Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount))>0));


=====QUERY=====
Presek_Sub_003
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(CreditAmount-DebitAmount) AS Sum
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)<>98) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)>Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text13 And (LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text15) And ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)<>3 And (LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)<>98))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;

FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)<>98) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<=Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text15) And ((LedgerEntry.PostingTypeCode)<>98))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId;

WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Parameters) Like Partner.PBPD_F) And ((LedgerEntry.Date)<Forms!Presek_Izvestaj!Text13))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Partner.CompanyId;

=====QUERY=====
PRESEK-BENEFITI
-----SQL-----
SELECT BenefitImport.BenefitImportRow AS [BW UNIT], Unit.Entrance, TipObjekta_1.Name AS [Unit Type], Unit.UnitTypeShortCode AS Unit, Unit.K1 AS Area, Partner.Name AS Invoice, oBJEKTIgpm.UnitTypeShortCode AS [Unit GPS], oBJEKTIgpm.K1 AS [Area GPS], oBJEKTIgpm.K2 AS [K2 GPS], MailGrupByIDK.MailGrupRacun, MailGrupByIDK.MailGrup
FROM MailGrupByIDK RIGHT JOIN (oBJEKTIgpm RIGHT JOIN (((Unit RIGHT JOIN BenefitImport ON Unit.UnitTypeShortCode = BenefitImport.BenefitImportRow) LEFT JOIN UnitType AS TipObjekta_1 ON Unit.UnitTypeId = TipObjekta_1.UnitTypeId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) ON oBJEKTIgpm.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId) ON MailGrupByIDK.PartnerId = Unit.PartnerId
ORDER BY Unit.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeCode, oBJEKTIgpm.UnitTypeCode;

=====QUERY=====
QRCODE
-----SQL-----
SELECT 'PR' AS K, '01' AS V, '1' AS C, TrZaQRcode(Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber) AS R, Left(Company.PrintName & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & Company.PostalCode,70) AS N, 'RSD' & Format(IIf(NZ(ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker,'')='',IIf(PreviousDebt+Total>0,PreviousDebt+Total,0),Total),'Fixed') AS I, Left(Partner & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & Address & ', ' & PostalCode,70) AS P, 221 AS SF, Left([Skustina_1].RemittanceInfo & ' ' & Label,35) AS S, QRCodePozivNaBroj(97,PaymentReference) AS RO, Invoice.InvoiceId AS SKIP_IDR, Company.Folder AS SKIP_FOLDER, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId AS SKIP_GR, Invoice.PaymentReference AS SKIP_PB
FROM ((InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Company AS Skustina_1 ON Invoice.CompanyId = Skustina_1.CompanyId) INNER JOIN Company ON Skustina_1.InvoiceIssuerId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceId)=30591));


=====QUERY=====
QRCODE_OPOMENA
-----SQL-----
SELECT 'PR' AS K, '01' AS V, '1' AS C, TrZaQRcode(Company.PrimaryBankAccountNumber) AS R, Left(Company.PrintName & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & Company.PostalCode,70) AS N, 'RSD' & Format(Reminder.Total,'Fixed') AS I, Left(Partner.Name & Chr(13) & Chr(10) & IIf(IsNull(Partner.Address),BuildingEntrance.Address,Partner.Address) & ', ' & IIf(IsNull(Partner.Address),[Skustina_1].PostalCode,Partner.PostalCode & ' ' & Partner.PartnerCity),70) AS P, 221 AS SF, Left(ReminderBatch.RemittanceInfo,35) AS S, QRCodePozivNaBroj(97,PaymentReference) AS RO, Reminder.ReminderId AS SKIP_IDR, Company.Folder AS SKIP_FOLDER, Reminder.ReminderBatchId AS SKIP_GR, Reminder.PaymentReference AS SKIP_PB
FROM (Company AS Skustina_1 RIGHT JOIN ((Company INNER JOIN ((Partner INNER JOIN (Reminder INNER JOIN SelectionBasket ON Reminder.ReminderId = SelectionBasket.TargetId) ON Partner.PartnerId = Reminder.PartnerAccountingId) INNER JOIN ReminderBatch ON Reminder.ReminderBatchId = ReminderBatch.ReminderBatchId) ON Company.CompanyId = ReminderBatch.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) ON Skustina_1.CompanyId = Unit.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN BuildingEntrance ON Unit.Entrance = BuildingEntrance.Entrance
WHERE (((Reminder.ReminderBatchId)=16) AND ((SelectionBasket.TypeIndex)=2));

=====QUERY=====
Query189
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, SortOrder, Name, CalculationType, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoiceId, K1xK2, K2xK3, K2xK4, K2xK5, VatRate, ExchangeRateNbs, UnitOfMeasure, UnitId )
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25, IIf(IsVatPayer<>0,SupplierInvoice.Vat,0) AS RacunPDV, InvoiceBatch.ExchangeRateNbs, CalculationType.UnitOfMeasure, Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitCount
FROM ((InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN ((Objekti_Za_Racun INNER JOIN (Partner INNER JOIN (SupplierInvoiceUnitType INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) ON Partner.PartnerId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId) ON (Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitTypeId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.UnitType) AND (Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId = SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) ON InvoiceBatch.Code = SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth) INNER JOIN TipRacunaDobavljac ON SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId = TipRacunaDobavljac.SortIndex) INNER JOIN SZ_IzdavalacRacuna ON Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId = SZ_IzdavalacRacuna.CompanyId
WHERE (((SupplierInvoice.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Is Null) AND ((TipRacunaDobavljac.GroupCode)=1) AND ((CalculationType.CalculationTypeId)<>99))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25, IIf(IsVatPayer<>0,SupplierInvoice.Vat,0), InvoiceBatch.ExchangeRateNbs, CalculationType.UnitOfMeasure, Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitCount, Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=13) AND ((Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId)=101))
ORDER BY Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth;

FROM (((((ReminderLine INNER JOIN Reminder ON ReminderLine.ReminderId = Reminder.ReminderId) INNER JOIN ReminderBatch ON Reminder.ReminderBatchId = ReminderBatch.ReminderBatchId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Reminder.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) LEFT JOIN Unit ON Partner.DeliveryUnitTypeCode = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN SelectionBasket ON Reminder.ReminderId = SelectionBasket.TargetId

=====QUERY=====
Query30
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( PartnerId, CompanyId, IssueDate, PlaceOfIssue, ServiceDate, Partner, PostalCode, Address, TaxId, StanjePredhodniDug, StanjeDug, Currency, PozivNaBrojPDug, ValueDate, PaymentPurpose, PaymentReference, InvoiceBatchId, UnitId, UnitName, PaymentPurpose2 )
SELECT InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, UCase(InvoiceBatch.Place) AS GrupaRacunaMesto, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, UCase(Partner.Name) AS KupacNaziv, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, KUPAC_TS.BLOK, KUPAC_TS.Balance, 'RSD' AS Expr1, Partner.PBPD, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, UnitType.InvoiceTitle AS Expr3, KontrolniBroj(97,InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear & InvoiceBatch.Month & '-' & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000')) & "-" & InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear & InvoiceBatch.Month & '-' & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000') AS ReferenceCode, InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.UnitId, UCase(Unit.Name) AS ObjNaziv, UCase(Unit.Name & ", " & Unit.Address & " " & Unit.Entrance & " - " & InvoiceBatch.Label) AS Expr2
FROM ((((InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Partner ON InvoiceLine.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN KUPAC_TS ON InvoiceLine.PartnerId = KUPAC_TS.ExchangeRateId) INNER JOIN Unit ON InvoiceLine.UnitId = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=49) AND ((InvoiceLine.InvoiceId)=0))
GROUP BY InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, UCase(InvoiceBatch.Place), InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, UCase(Partner.Name), Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, KUPAC_TS.BLOK, KUPAC_TS.Balance, 'RSD', Partner.PBPD, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, UnitType.InvoiceTitle, KontrolniBroj(97,InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear & InvoiceBatch.Month & '-' & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000')) & "-" & InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear & InvoiceBatch.Month & '-' & Format(InvoiceLine.PartnerId,'0000'), InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.UnitId, UCase(Unit.Name), UCase(Unit.Name & ", " & Unit.Address & " " & Unit.Entrance & " - " & InvoiceBatch.Label);


=====QUERY=====
Query300
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "ODRŽAVANJE TR, PROVIZIJE ZA " & GetYYMMfromDate(Date,"-",4) AS Name, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, PoslednjiDanMesecaOdDatuma(Date) AS PDM, FormatDatumLikeSort(PoslednjiDanMesecaOdDatuma(Date)) & " TR " & " " & GetYYMMfromDate(Date,"-",4) AS SequenceNumber, GetYYMMfromDate(Date) AS PeriodYyMm
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "ODRŽAVANJE TR, PROVIZIJE ZA " & GetYYMMfromDate(Date,"-",4), LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, PoslednjiDanMesecaOdDatuma(Date), FormatDatumLikeSort(PoslednjiDanMesecaOdDatuma(Date)) & " TR " & " " & GetYYMMfromDate(Date,"-",4), GetYYMMfromDate(Date)
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId)=9003));

Query301
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, "R-" & Code AS Document, InvoiceBatch.Label, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Invoice.InvoiceId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount)-Total,2) AS Razlika, Invoice.Total
FROM LedgerEntry INNER JOIN (InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) ON LedgerEntry.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((Invoice.IsCancelled)=False) AND ((LedgerEntry.Date) Between [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, "R-" & Code, InvoiceBatch.Label, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate, Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.Total
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate) Between [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumOd] And [Forms]![Izvestaji]![txtDatumDo]) AND ((Round(Sum(DebitAmount)-Total,2))<>0))
ORDER BY InvoiceBatch.IssueDate;


=====QUERY=====
Query302
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=3)) OR (((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=4));


=====QUERY=====
Query303
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Invoice.Total, Invoice.InvoiceId, Round(Total,2)-Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2) AS kontrola
FROM Invoice INNER JOIN LedgerEntry ON Invoice.InvoiceId = LedgerEntry.InvoiceId
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Invoice.Total, Invoice.InvoiceId
HAVING (((Round(Total,2)-Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2))<>0));


=====QUERY=====
Query304
-----SQL-----
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef
FROM Invoice RIGHT JOIN LedgerEntry ON Invoice.InvoiceId = LedgerEntry.InvoiceId
GROUP BY Invoice.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef
HAVING (((Invoice.InvoiceId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
Query305
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS BaseAmount
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SubAccount
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber)=167));


=====QUERY=====
Query306
-----SQL-----
SELECT Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Year(Date) AS Expr1, Month(Date) AS Expr2, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "ODRŽAVANJE TR, PROVIZIJE ZA " & Year(Date) & "-" & Format(Month(Date),"00") AS INFO, Last(LedgerEntry.Date) AS LastOfDATUM, DateAdded("d",-1,DateAdded("m",1,Year(Date) & "-" & Month(Date) & "-" & 1)) AS DP
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Year(Date), Month(Date), LedgerEntry.CompanyId, "ODRŽAVANJE TR, PROVIZIJE ZA " & Year(Date) & "-" & Format(Month(Date),"00"), DateAdded("d",-1,DateAdded("m",1,Year(Date) & "-" & Month(Date) & "-" & 1))
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId)=9003));


=====QUERY=====
Query307
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, Round(Sum(CreditAmount),2) AS SP, Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2) AS SD, Count(LedgerEntry.LedgerEntryId) AS BrojStavki
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like '204*'))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId
HAVING (((Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2))<>0) AND ((Round(Sum(DebitAmount),2))>-0.01));

Query308

SELECT InvoiceBatch.Month, InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear, InvoiceBatch.CompanyId, Unit.UnitTypeId, Unit.UnitId, Unit.Name

WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.Month)="02") AND ((InvoiceBatch.FiscalYear)="2026") AND ((InvoiceBatch.CompanyId)=231) AND ((Unit.UnitTypeId)=4));

Query308-2

=====QUERY=====
Query310
-----SQL-----
SELECT PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId
FROM PenaltyInterestStaging
WHERE (((PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId)=33))
GROUP BY PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId;


=====QUERY=====
Query311
-----SQL-----
UPDATE (SELECT PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId, PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId
FROM PenaltyInterestStaging
WHERE (((PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId)=33))
GROUP BY PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId, PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId
)  AS ZKGRUP INNER JOIN Invoice ON (ZKGRUP.PartnerAccountingId = Invoice.PartnerId) AND (ZKGRUP.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) SET Invoice.InterestAmount = [ZKGRUP].[SumOfDIZNOS];


=====QUERY=====
Query312
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Invoice SET Invoice.Total = InvoiceTotal+InterestAmount
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=33));


=====QUERY=====
Query313
-----SQL-----
UPDATE Invoice INNER JOIN (SELECT
        PartnerAccountingId,
        InvoiceBatchId,
        Sum(DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS
    FROM PenaltyInterestStaging
    WHERE InvoiceBatchId = 34
    GROUP BY PartnerAccountingId, InvoiceBatchId
)  AS ZKGRUP ON (Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = ZKGRUP.InvoiceBatchId) AND (Invoice.PartnerId = ZKGRUP.PartnerAccountingId) SET Invoice.InterestAmount = ZKGRUP.SumOfDIZNOS;


=====QUERY=====
Query314
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, IssueDate, ServiceDate, ValueDate, TransactionDate, PlaceOfIssue, Partner, PostalCode, Address, TaxId, RegistrationNumber, PreviousDebt, PaymentSlipAmount, Total, InvoiceTotal, InterestAmount, BalanceAsOfDate, InvoiceLayout, Location )
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Partner.GroupInvoiceTagId, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, Kupac_1.Name, Kupac_1.PostalCode, Kupac_1.Address, Kupac_1.TaxId, Kupac_1.TaxId, Nz([Suma0],0) AS PDD, Nz([Suma98],0) AS PDN, Sum(Invoice.Total) AS SumOfUkupno, Sum(Invoice.InvoiceTotal) AS SumOfUR, Sum(Invoice.InterestAmount) AS SumOfKI, InvoiceBatch.BalanceAsOfDate, 'GR' AS InvoiceLayout, Company.Folder
FROM (Kupac_DUG RIGHT JOIN (Partner AS Kupac_1 INNER JOIN ((InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN Invoice ON InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId = Invoice.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Partner ON Invoice.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) ON Kupac_1.PartnerId = Partner.GroupInvoiceTagId) ON Kupac_DUG.PartnerAccountingId = Kupac_1.PartnerId) INNER JOIN Company ON InvoiceBatch.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Partner.GroupInvoiceTagId, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.TransactionDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, Kupac_1.Name, Kupac_1.PostalCode, Kupac_1.Address, Kupac_1.TaxId, InvoiceBatch.BalanceAsOfDate, Company.Folder, Kupac_DUG.Suma0, Kupac_DUG.Suma98
HAVING (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=35) AND (Not (Partner.GroupInvoiceTagId) Is Null));


=====QUERY=====
Query315
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, DebitAmount, LineTypeId, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, DocumentRef, DueDate, Parameters, InvoiceId, SubAccount, AccountCode )
SELECT " & novNalog & " AS JournalEntry, PenaltyInterestStaging.Account, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount, PenaltyInterestStaging.LineItemType, PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId, 'R-' & PenaltyInterestStaging.Parameters AS DocumentRef, PenaltyInterestStaging.ValueDate, Replace(Invoice.PaymentReference,'-','') AS ReferenceCode, PenaltyInterestStaging.SupplierInvoiceId, PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode, PenaltyInterestStaging.PartnerAccountingId
FROM PenaltyInterestStaging INNER JOIN Invoice ON PenaltyInterestStaging.SupplierInvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId
WHERE (((PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount)<>0) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId)=101) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId)=164));

=====QUERY=====
Query317
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, DocumentRef, DueDate, SubAccount, AccountCode )
SELECT " & novNalog & " AS JournalEntry, '4909' AS Account, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, PenaltyInterestStaging.LineItemType, PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, 0 AS PartnerAccountingId, 'R-' & PenaltyInterestStaging.Parameters AS DocumentRef, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode, SubAccountDefaultSupplier.DefaultSupplierPartnerAccountingId
FROM (PenaltyInterestStaging INNER JOIN Invoice ON PenaltyInterestStaging.SupplierInvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN SubAccountDefaultSupplier ON (PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId = SubAccountDefaultSupplier.CompanyId) AND (PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode = SubAccountDefaultSupplier.Account)
GROUP BY " & novNalog & ", '4909', PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.LineItemType, PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, 0, 'R-' & PenaltyInterestStaging.Parameters, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode, SubAccountDefaultSupplier.DefaultSupplierPartnerAccountingId, PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId
HAVING (((Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount))<>0) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId)=101) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId)=164));

Query33

INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( Area, [MAX-CN], [MAX-Area], [MAX-Sum], [MAX-KOEF], KOEF, CalculationType, Name, SortOrder, Text, ExpenseGroup )
SELECT Unit.Quantity, Objekat_Sume.[MAX-CN], Objekat_Sume.[MAX-Area], ExpenseContract.Value, Objekat_Sume.[MAX-KO], Unit.Coefficient, ExpenseContract.CalculationType, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.SortOrder, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Text, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseGroup
FROM (ExpenseContract RIGHT JOIN (Objekat_Sume INNER JOIN ((Unit INNER JOIN ExpenseTypeSubtype ON Unit.UnitTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.UnitTypeReportLabel) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Objekat_Sume.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON ExpenseContract.ExpenseTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON ExpenseContract.SupplierPartnerAccountingId=Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=0))
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name;

FROM (Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT RIGHT JOIN (Objekat_Sume INNER JOIN ((Unit INNER JOIN ExpenseTypeSubtype ON Unit.UnitTypeId = ExpenseTypeSubtype.UnitTypeReportLabel) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Objekat_Sume.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.ExpenseTypeId = ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId) LEFT JOIN Partner ON Troskovi_Ugovor_FLT.SupplierPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Unit.Status)=0))
ORDER BY UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name;


=====QUERY=====
Query45
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, DocumentRef, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, Parameters )
SELECT 1 AS mNalog, '4330' & Format(IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId),'0000') AS mKonto, #12/30/1899# AS mDatum, Round(Sum(IIf([IznosE]>0,[IznosE]*ExchangeRateNbs,[IznosD])),2) AS Total, 4 AS mTip, 'UF' & 114 AS mDOK, '27' AS mSK, Format(IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId),'0000') AS Account, 114 AS mPar
FROM ExpenseInvoice INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceMonth=InvoiceBatch.Code
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=111))
GROUP BY ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceNumber, IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId), [mDatum]
HAVING (((Round(Sum(IIf([IznosE]>0,[IznosE]*ExchangeRateNbs,[IznosD])),2))>0));


=====QUERY=====
Query46
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, DocumentRef, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, Parameters )
SELECT 5280 AS mNalog, '4330' & Format(IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId),'0000') AS mKonto, #1/1/2014# AS mDatum, Round(Sum(IIf([IznosE]>0,[IznosE]*ExchangeRateNbs,[IznosD])),2) AS Total, 4 AS mTip, 'UF' & 114 AS mDOK, '27' AS mSK, Format(IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId),'0000') AS Account, 114 AS mPar
FROM ExpenseInvoice INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceMonth=InvoiceBatch.Code
WHERE (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=111))
GROUP BY ExpenseInvoice.InvoiceNumber, 1, IIf(SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId=0,9917,SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId), [mDatum]
HAVING (((Sum(IIf([IznosE]>0,[IznosE]*ExchangeRateNbs,[IznosD])))>0));

SELECT 14 AS JournalEntry, '2040' & Invoice.PartnerId AS Account, Invoice.IssueDate, InvoiceLine.TotalRsd, 3 AS Type, Invoice.CompanyId, Invoice.PartnerId, 'IF' & PaymentReference AS DocumentRef, Invoice.ValueDate, Replace(PaymentReference,'-','') AS ReferenceCode, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, Invoice.InvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.CollectionPriority, Invoice.PartnerId

Query65

INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, DocumentRef, Parameters, SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceId, PostingCode, AccountCode )
SELECT 14 AS JournalEntry, '4350' & InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId AS Account, Invoice.IssueDate, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, 3 AS Type, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, PostingCode & '-' & InvoiceMonth AS DocumentRef, PostingCode & '-' & InvoiceMonth AS ReferenceCode, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId

GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth

Query78
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, SortOrder, Name, CalculationType, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoiceId, K1xK2, K2xK3, K2xK4, K2xK5 )
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN ((Objekti_Za_Racun INNER JOIN (Partner INNER JOIN (SupplierInvoiceUnitType INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) ON Partner.PartnerId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId) ON (Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitTypeId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.UnitType) AND (Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId = SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) ON InvoiceBatch.Code = SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=597) AND ((Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId)=118))
ORDER BY Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth;


=====QUERY=====
Query79
-----SQL-----
UPDATE (Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON (Invoice.CompanyId = InvoiceLine.CompanyId) AND (Invoice.PartnerId = InvoiceLine.PartnerId) AND (Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId SET InvoiceLine.InvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId, Invoice.SequenceNumber = Invoice.CompanyId & '-' & Format(Invoice.PartnerId) & '-' & Code & '-' & ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker, Invoice.PaymentReference = KontrolniBroj(97,Invoice.CompanyId & '-' & Format(Invoice.PartnerId) & '-' & Code & '-' & ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) & '-' & Invoice.CompanyId & '-' & Invoice.PartnerId & '-' & Code & '-' & ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker
WHERE (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=724) AND ((Invoice.CompanyId)=104));


=====QUERY=====
Query8
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( Objekat, InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, Quantity, UnitOfMeasure, JO, SortOrder )
SELECT 'Rezervni fond' AS mRF, " & linkGrupeRacuna & " AS GRPRC, Unit.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O, 'kom' AS mJM, InvestmentMaintenanceReserve/[NBSKURS] AS EURO, 10 AS mSort
FROM (Unit LEFT JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId=Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId
GROUP BY 'Rezervni fond', " & linkGrupeRacuna & ", Unit.PartnerId, Unit.CompanyId, 'kom', InvestmentMaintenanceReserve/[NBSKURS], 10, Unit.Status
HAVING (((InvestmentMaintenanceReserve/[NBSKURS])>0) AND ((Unit.Status)=0));


=====QUERY=====
Query80
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, SortOrder, Name, CalculationType, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoiceId, K1xK2, K2xK3, K2xK4, K2xK5 )
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN ((Objekti_Za_Racun INNER JOIN (Partner INNER JOIN (SupplierInvoiceUnitType INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) ON Partner.PartnerId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId) ON (Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitTypeId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.UnitType) AND (Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId = SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) ON InvoiceBatch.Code = SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
WHERE (((SupplierInvoice.ExtraordinaryInvoiceMarker) Is Null))
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceNumber, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.K21, Objekti_Za_Racun.K23, Objekti_Za_Racun.K24, Objekti_Za_Racun.K25, Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=733) AND ((Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId)=104))
ORDER BY Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth;

Query84
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.Account, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date
FROM LedgerEntry
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=101))
GROUP BY LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date

=====QUERY=====
Query85
-----SQL-----
SELECT SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, Sum(GK_1.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS1, Sum(GK_1.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS1, Sum(GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SumOfDIZNOS) AS SumOfSumOfDIZNOS, Sum(GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SumOfPIZNOS) AS SumOfSumOfPIZNOS, GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SubAccount
FROM (LedgerEntry AS GK_1 INNER JOIN (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON (SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = LedgerEntry.CompanyId) AND (LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId)) ON (GK_1.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) AND (GK_1.CompanyId = SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)) INNER JOIN GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI ON (SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SupplierInvoiceId) AND (SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.CompanyId)
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.Account) Like "2040*") AND ((GK_1.Account) Like "4350*"))
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.CompanyId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, GK_FILTER_DOBAVLJACI.SubAccount
HAVING (((SupplierInvoice.CompanyId)=101));

Query86

FROM LedgerEntry
