=====QUERY=====
Query87
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, DocumentRef, Parameters, SupplierInvoiceId, PostingCode, AccountCode, SubAccount, InboundInvoiceId )
SELECT 5550 AS JournalEntry, Partner.Account AS Account, Invoice.IssueDate, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD, 3 AS Type, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, 'R-' & InvoiceMonth AS DocumentRef, SupplierInvoice.PaymentReference, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM ((Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId = InvoiceLine.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId
GROUP BY 5550, Partner.Account, Invoice.IssueDate, 3, Invoice.CompanyId, InvoiceLine.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, 'R-' & InvoiceMonth, SupplierInvoice.PaymentReference, InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId, SupplierInvoice.PostingCode, SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, SupplierInvoice.PostingAccount, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
HAVING (((Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd))<>0) AND ((Invoice.CompanyId)=152) AND ((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=786));

=====QUERY=====
Query89
-----SQL-----
SELECT '' AS LedgerEntryId, '' AS JournalEntryNumber, '' AS Date, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS Credit, Sum(LedgerEntry.DebitAmount) AS Debit, Round(Sum(DebitAmount-CreditAmount),2) AS SPD, IIf(PostingTypeCode>0,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId) AS TS, LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.Priority
FROM LedgerEntry
GROUP BY IIf(PostingTypeCode>0,PostingTypeCode,LineTypeId), LedgerEntry.AccountCode, LedgerEntry.PostingCode, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InvoiceId, LedgerEntry.InboundInvoiceId, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Parameters, LedgerEntry.Priority
HAVING (((LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId)=1006) AND ((LedgerEntry.CompanyId)=101) AND ((LedgerEntry.DocumentRef) Like 'R-1912'))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, LedgerEntry.Priority;

FROM ((SupplierInvoice INNER JOIN Partner ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) INNER JOIN TipRacunaDobavljac ON SupplierInvoice.DocumentTypeId = TipRacunaDobavljac.SortIndex

SELECT Company.Name AS [prodavac           (skupština stanara)], Invoice.Partner, Invoice.ServiceDate AS [Date pružanja usluge], Invoice.Total AS [Zaduženje za Month], Invoice.IssueDate AS [Date izdavanja racuna], Invoice.PartnerId AS Id, Invoice.PaymentReference AS [Model i poziv na Number], Invoice.PozivNaBrojPDug AS [Poziv na Number za Debt]

=====QUERY=====
RacunRacunStavke
-----SQL-----
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Unit.UnitTypeId, InvoiceBatch.Code, InvoiceLine.Name, InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId, InvoiceLine.DobAlias, InvoiceLine.Total, ExpenseTypeSubtype.Name, ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeDescription, InvoiceLine.SortOrder, UnitName & " TC" & Entrance AS [ON], Unit.Entrance
FROM (((Invoice INNER JOIN InvoiceLine ON Invoice.InvoiceId=InvoiceLine.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON Invoice.InvoiceBatchId=InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN ExpenseTypeSubtype ON InvoiceLine.ExpenseTypeId=ExpenseTypeSubtype.ExpenseTypeId) INNER JOIN Unit ON Invoice.UnitId=Unit.UnitId
WHERE (((Unit.UnitTypeId)=3) AND ((InvoiceBatch.Code)="1013"))
ORDER BY Unit.Entrance, Invoice.UnitName;


=====QUERY=====
RacunRacunStavke_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(RacunRacunStavke.Total) AS Sum
SELECT RacunRacunStavke.SortOrder, RacunRacunStavke.ExpenseTypeDescription
FROM RacunRacunStavke
GROUP BY RacunRacunStavke.SortOrder, RacunRacunStavke.ExpenseTypeDescription
ORDER BY RacunRacunStavke.SortOrder
PIVOT RacunRacunStavke.[ON];


=====QUERY=====
RacunRacunStavke_Crosstab-2
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(RacunRacunStavke.Total) AS Sum
SELECT RacunRacunStavke.Entrance, RacunRacunStavke.[ON]
FROM RacunRacunStavke
GROUP BY RacunRacunStavke.Entrance, RacunRacunStavke.[ON]
ORDER BY RacunRacunStavke.Entrance, RacunRacunStavke.[ON], RacunRacunStavke.SortOrder
PIVOT RacunRacunStavke.SortOrder;

=====QUERY=====
REPORT_KV_PROSEK
-----SQL-----
SELECT Unit.CompanyId, Company.Building, Count(Unit.UnitId) AS CountOfID_O, UnitType.Name, Sum(Unit.K1) AS SumOfK1, Sum(Unit.NetArea) AS SumOfnetoKV, Sum(Unit.Terrace) AS SumOfterasa, Sum(Unit.NetAreaWithTerrace) AS SumOfnetoKVsaTerasom
FROM (Unit INNER JOIN Company ON Unit.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId
GROUP BY Unit.CompanyId, Company.Building, UnitType.Name, UnitType.SortOrder
ORDER BY Unit.CompanyId, UnitType.SortOrder;


=====QUERY=====
REVIEW-IZVOD-ISPLATE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Company.Building, BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.CompanyId, BankStatementLine.PayerRecipientName, BankStatementLine.BankAccountNumber, BankStatementLine.RemittanceInfo, BankStatementLine.Debit, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.SubAccount, SubAccount.Name, LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM (LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN (Company INNER JOIN BankStatementLine ON Company.CompanyId = BankStatementLine.CompanyId) ON LedgerEntry.BankStatementLineId = BankStatementLine.Id) LEFT JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode
WHERE (((BankStatementLine.Debit)>0))
ORDER BY BankStatementLine.ExecutionDate, BankStatementLine.CompanyId;


=====QUERY=====
REVIEW-PROMENE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Events.EventId, Events.DateOfRequest, Events.DateOfExecution, Company.Building AS CompanyId, Events.ExchangeRateId, Partner.Name, Events.UnitId, Events.Description, Events.PreviousValue, Events.NewValue, Events.FieldsRelated, Events.RequestType, Events.RequestBy, Events.RequestThrough, ConcatRelated("SifraPD","Objekti","lnk_ID_K = " & Nz(PartnerId,-1)) AS PD
FROM (Partner LEFT JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId) RIGHT JOIN Events ON Partner.PartnerId = Events.ExchangeRateId;

=====QUERY=====
REVIEW-RACUNA-NEFAKTURISESE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Unit.Name, Unit.Entrance, UnitType.Name, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Unit.UnitTypeShortCode, Unit.K1, Unit.K2, Unit.Note

=====QUERY=====
RunMeKillMe
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO LedgerEntry ( JournalEntryNumber, Account, Date, CreditAmount, LineTypeId, CompanyId, PartnerAccountingId, DocumentRef, DueDate, SubAccount, AccountCode )
SELECT 4282 AS JournalEntry, '4909' AS Account, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount) AS SumOfDIZNOS, PenaltyInterestStaging.LineItemType, PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, 0 AS PartnerAccountingId, 'R-' & PenaltyInterestStaging.Parameters AS DocumentRef, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode, SubAccountDefaultSupplier.DefaultSupplierPartnerAccountingId
FROM (PenaltyInterestStaging INNER JOIN Invoice ON PenaltyInterestStaging.SupplierInvoiceId = Invoice.InvoiceId) INNER JOIN SubAccountDefaultSupplier ON (PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId = SubAccountDefaultSupplier.CompanyId) AND (PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode = SubAccountDefaultSupplier.Account)
GROUP BY 4282, '4909', PenaltyInterestStaging.LineItemType, PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId, 0, 'R-' & PenaltyInterestStaging.Parameters, PenaltyInterestStaging.Date, PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode, SubAccountDefaultSupplier.DefaultSupplierPartnerAccountingId, PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId
HAVING (((Sum(PenaltyInterestStaging.DebitAmount))<>0) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId)=101) AND ((PenaltyInterestStaging.InvoiceBatchId)=164));

SELECT Partner.LegacyMasterId, Unit.Name, Unit.UnitId, Partner.PartnerId, Unit.GarageSpotNumber, UnitType.Name, Objekti_1.Entrance, UnitType.SortOrder, Unit.UnitTypeCode, Objekti_1.Name, TipObjekta_1.SortOrder, Objekti_1.UnitTypeCode, Partner.Name

FROM ((((Unit INNER JOIN Partner ON Unit.PartnerId = Partner.LegacyMasterId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Unit AS Objekti_1 ON Partner.PartnerId = Objekti_1.PartnerId) INNER JOIN UnitType AS TipObjekta_1 ON Objekti_1.UnitTypeId = TipObjekta_1.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN Invoice ON Partner.PartnerId = Invoice.PartnerId

=====QUERY=====
STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PBPD_F, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.Parameters = Partner.PBPD_F) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.DebitAmount)=0) AND ((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)<>0))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date;


=====QUERY=====
STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-STANJE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PBPD_F, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name, Sum(Round(Round(DebitAmount,2)-Round(CreditAmount,2),2)) AS Balance
FROM (LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.Parameters=Partner.PBPD_F) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY Partner.PBPD_F, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name;


=====QUERY=====
STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-SVE
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PBPD_F, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, [STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance].Balance
FROM ((LedgerEntry RIGHT JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.Parameters=Partner.PBPD_F) LEFT JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId) RIGHT JOIN [STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance] ON Partner.PartnerId=[STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance].PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)<>0) AND ((LedgerEntry.DebitAmount)=0))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date;


=====QUERY=====
STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-SVE-BEZ0
-----SQL-----
SELECT Partner.PBPD_F, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Company.Name, LedgerEntry.Date, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, [STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance].Balance
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.Parameters=Partner.PBPD_F) INNER JOIN Company ON Partner.CompanyId=Company.CompanyId) INNER JOIN [STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance] ON Partner.PartnerId=[STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance].PartnerId
WHERE (((LedgerEntry.CreditAmount)<>0) AND (([STT001-UPLATE-ZA-BLOK-Balance].Balance)>0.01) AND ((LedgerEntry.DebitAmount)=0))
ORDER BY LedgerEntry.Date;


=====QUERY=====

-----SQL-----
SELECT SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, LedgerEntry.Account, LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber, LedgerEntry.Date, LineItemType.LineItemType, Partner.Name, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, LedgerEntry.Note, LedgerEntry.DebitAmount, LedgerEntry.CreditAmount, LedgerEntry.DocumentRef, DebitAmount-CreditAmount AS BaseAmount, LedgerEntry.Parameters, JournalEntry.Description
FROM (((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SubAccount ON LedgerEntry.SubAccount = SubAccount.SubAccountCode) INNER JOIN LineItemType ON LedgerEntry.LineTypeId = LineItemType.LineItemTypeId) INNER JOIN Partner ON LedgerEntry.PartnerAccountingId = Partner.PartnerId) INNER JOIN JournalEntry ON LedgerEntry.JournalEntryNumber = JournalEntry.JournalEntryNumber

=====QUERY=====
TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA
-----SQL-----
SELECT LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Building, LedgerEntry.Date, LineItemType.LineItemType, Sum(LedgerEntry.CreditAmount) AS SumOfPIZNOS, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId
FROM ((LedgerEntry INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON LedgerEntry.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN LineItemType ON LedgerEntry.LineTypeId = LineItemType.LineItemTypeId) INNER JOIN Company ON LedgerEntry.CompanyId = Company.CompanyId
GROUP BY SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, LedgerEntry.CompanyId, Company.Building, LedgerEntry.Date, LineItemType.LineItemType, LedgerEntry.LineTypeId
HAVING (((SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId)=9034) AND ((LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=1 Or (LedgerEntry.LineTypeId)=8));


=====QUERY=====
TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA_Crosstab
-----SQL-----
TRANSFORM Sum(TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.[SumOfPIZNOS]) AS SumOfSumOfPIZNOS
SELECT TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.Date, TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.LineItemType, Sum(TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.[SumOfPIZNOS]) AS [Total Of SumOfPIZNOS]
FROM TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA
GROUP BY TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.Date, TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.LineItemType
PIVOT TEMP_GK_KNJIZENJE_ZA_DOB_PO_DANIMA.Building;

=====QUERY=====
TEMP-R1-BRISI
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO InvoiceLine ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, K1, K2, K3, K4, K5, Name, CalculationType, SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId, SupplierInvoiceId )
SELECT InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId
FROM InvoiceBatch INNER JOIN ((Objekti_Za_Racun INNER JOIN (Partner INNER JOIN (SupplierInvoiceUnitType INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON SupplierInvoiceUnitType.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) ON Partner.PartnerId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierAccountPartnerAccountingId) ON (SupplierInvoice.CompanyId = Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId) AND (Objekti_Za_Racun.UnitTypeId = SupplierInvoiceUnitType.UnitType)) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) ON InvoiceBatch.Label = SupplierInvoice.InvoiceMonth
GROUP BY InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId, Objekti_Za_Racun.PartnerId, Objekti_Za_Racun.CompanyId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK1, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK2, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK3, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK4, Objekti_Za_Racun.SumOfK5, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceName, CalculationType.CalculationTypeId, Partner.PartnerId, SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId, Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.SortMarkerYYYYMM
HAVING (((InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId)=216))
ORDER BY Objekti_Za_Racun.SortOrder, SupplierInvoice.SortMarkerYYYYMM;


=====QUERY=====
TEMP-R2-BRISI
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( InvoiceBatchId, PartnerId, CompanyId, IssueDate, ServiceDate, ValueDate, PlaceOfIssue, Partner, PostalCode, Address, TaxId, PreviousDebt, Total )
SELECT InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, Nz(Sum,0) AS PD, Sum(InvoiceLine.TotalRsd) AS SumOfUkupnoRSD
FROM ((InvoiceLine INNER JOIN InvoiceBatch ON InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId = InvoiceBatch.InvoiceBatchId) INNER JOIN Partner ON InvoiceLine.PartnerId = Partner.PartnerId) LEFT JOIN Kupac_DUG ON InvoiceLine.PartnerId = Kupac_DUG.PartnerAccountingId
GROUP BY InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId, InvoiceLine.PartnerId, InvoiceLine.CompanyId, InvoiceBatch.IssueDate, InvoiceBatch.ServiceDate, InvoiceBatch.ValueDate, InvoiceBatch.Place, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.TaxId, Nz(Sum,0)
HAVING (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=216));


=====QUERY=====
TEMP-R3-BRISI
-----SQL-----
SELECT CalculationType.Quantity, CalculationType.Amount, Objekat_Sume_Po_TipovimaIzRacuna.*, SupplierInvoice.InvoiceAmountRsd, SupplierInvoice.AmountByCoefficientRsd, InvoiceLine.*
FROM ((InvoiceLine INNER JOIN SupplierInvoice ON InvoiceLine.SupplierInvoiceId = SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId) INNER JOIN CalculationType ON SupplierInvoice.CalculationType = CalculationType.CalculationTypeId) INNER JOIN Objekat_Sume_Po_TipovimaIzRacuna ON SupplierInvoice.SupplierInvoiceId = Objekat_Sume_Po_TipovimaIzRacuna.SupplierInvoiceId
WHERE (((InvoiceLine.InvoiceBatchId)=216));

=====QUERY=====
TK_SPISAK_KONTA
-----SQL-----

FROM ((TK1 INNER JOIN TK2 ON TK1.SubAccountCode = TK2.ParentAccount) INNER JOIN TK3 ON TK2.SubAccountCode = TK3.ParentAccount) INNER JOIN TK4 ON TK3.SubAccountCode = TK4.ParentAccount
ORDER BY TK4.SubAccountCode;

=====QUERY=====
TK2
-----SQL-----
SELECT SubAccount.SubAccountCode, SubAccount.Name, Len(SubAccountCode) AS Level, Left(SubAccountCode,1) AS ParentAccount
FROM SubAccount
WHERE (((Len(SubAccountCode))=2));


=====QUERY=====

=====QUERY=====
TKONTO
-----SQL-----
SELECT SubAccount.SubAccountCode, IIf(Nz(CostAllocationTarget,"")<>"",CostAllocationTarget,SubAccountCode) AS TKONTO, Left(IIf(Nz(CostAllocationTarget,"")<>"",CostAllocationTarget,SubAccountCode),2) AS GRUPA1, Left(IIf(Nz(CostAllocationTarget,"")<>"",CostAllocationTarget,SubAccountCode),4) AS GRUPA4, SubAccount.CostAllocationTarget
FROM SubAccount
WHERE (((Len(SubAccountCode))=5));


=====QUERY=====
tmpQuery

SELECT Company.CompanyId, Company.Name, Partner.PartnerId, Partner.Name, Partner.RegistrationNumber, Unit.Name, Unit.Quantity, Unit.UnitOfMeasure

=====QUERY=====

FROM Troskovi_Qry
GROUP BY Troskovi_Qry.Name
PIVOT Troskovi_Qry.Contract;

PIVOT Troskovi_Qry.Contract;

=====QUERY=====
ZBIRNIRACUN-KREIRAJ
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( InvoiceBatchId, IssueDate, PlaceOfIssue, ServiceDate, PartnerId, CompanyId, Partner, PostalCode, Address, PIB_Naziv, TaxId, Total, StanjeDug, TotalDebt, ValueDate, PaymentPurpose, Note, UpravljanjeZgradom, PaymentReference )
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.PlaceOfIssue, Invoice.ServiceDate, Partner.PartnerId, Invoice.CompanyId, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.PIB_N, Partner.TaxId, Sum(Invoice.Total*[IG_V]) AS [NR-Total], KUPAC_TS.Balance, KUPAC_TS.Balance+(Sum(Invoice.Total*[IG_V])) AS UD, Invoice.ValueDate, Invoice.PaymentPurpose, Invoice.Note, Invoice.UpravljanjeZgradom, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId) & Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId AS ReferenceTag
FROM (Partner INNER JOIN ((Invoice INNER JOIN Unit ON Invoice.UnitId = Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId = UnitType.UnitTypeId) ON Partner.PartnerId = UnitType.IgAccount) INNER JOIN KUPAC_TS ON Partner.PartnerId = KUPAC_TS.ExchangeRateId
WHERE (((Invoice.QrCodeFlag)=1))
GROUP BY Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.PlaceOfIssue, Invoice.ServiceDate, Partner.PartnerId, Invoice.CompanyId, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.PIB_N, Partner.TaxId, KUPAC_TS.Balance, Invoice.ValueDate, Invoice.PaymentPurpose, Invoice.Note, Invoice.UpravljanjeZgradom, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId) & Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId, Unit.UnitTypeId
HAVING (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=117));


=====QUERY=====
ZBIRNIRACUN-KREIRAJ2
-----SQL-----
INSERT INTO Invoice ( InvoiceBatchId, IssueDate, PlaceOfIssue, ServiceDate, PartnerId, CompanyId, Partner, PostalCode, Address, PIB_Naziv, TaxId, Total, StanjeDug, TotalDebt, ValueDate, PaymentPurpose, Note, UpravljanjeZgradom, PaymentReference )
SELECT Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.PlaceOfIssue, Invoice.ServiceDate, Partner.PartnerId, Invoice.CompanyId, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.PIB_N, Partner.TaxId, Sum(Invoice.Total*[CB_V]) AS [NR-Total], KUPAC_TS.Balance, KUPAC_TS.Balance+(Sum(Invoice.Total*[CB_V])) AS UD, Invoice.ValueDate, Invoice.PaymentPurpose, Invoice.Note, Invoice.UpravljanjeZgradom, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId) & Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId AS ReferenceTag
FROM ((Invoice INNER JOIN Unit ON Invoice.UnitId=Unit.UnitId) INNER JOIN UnitType ON Unit.UnitTypeId=UnitType.UnitTypeId) INNER JOIN (Partner INNER JOIN KUPAC_TS ON Partner.PartnerId=KUPAC_TS.ExchangeRateId) ON UnitType.CbAccount=Partner.PartnerId
WHERE (((Invoice.QrCodeFlag)=1))
GROUP BY Invoice.InvoiceBatchId, Invoice.IssueDate, Invoice.PlaceOfIssue, Invoice.ServiceDate, Partner.PartnerId, Invoice.CompanyId, Partner.Name, Partner.PostalCode, Partner.Address, Partner.PIB_N, Partner.TaxId, KUPAC_TS.Balance, Invoice.ValueDate, Invoice.PaymentPurpose, Invoice.Note, Invoice.UpravljanjeZgradom, KontrolniBroj(97,Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId) & Mid(PaymentReference,3,7) & Partner.PartnerId, Unit.UnitTypeId
HAVING (((Invoice.InvoiceBatchId)=117));

FROM SubAccountDefaultSupplier RIGHT JOIN PenaltyInterestStaging ON (SubAccountDefaultSupplier.CompanyId = PenaltyInterestStaging.CompanyId) AND (SubAccountDefaultSupplier.Account = PenaltyInterestStaging.SubAccountCode)
